```json
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c01",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s1",
      "quote": "Le tamarro est un animal imaginaire qui figure largement dans les mythologies pyrénéennes catalanes, et même au-delà puisqu'on retrouve la forme occitane tamarre ou tamarro dans l'Aubrac et l'Aveyron.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tamarro adalah hewan khayalan yang banyak muncul dalam mitologi Pyrenees Katalan, bahkan lebih jauh, karena bentuk Oksitan tamarre atau tamarro dikenal di Aubrac dan Aveyron.",
        "en": "The tamarro is an imaginary animal widespread in Catalan Pyrenean mythology and beyond, since the Occitan form tamarre or tamarro is found in Aubrac and Aveyron."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c02",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s2",
      "quote": "Segons la llegenda, era una bèstia esmunyedissa, que habitava les valls pirinenques i romania oculta, de manera que era gairebé impossible de caçar-la.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, ia binatang licin yang menghuni lembah-lembah Pyrenees dan selalu bersembunyi, sehingga hampir mustahil diburu.",
        "en": "By the legend it was an elusive beast living in the Pyrenean valleys and staying hidden, so it was almost impossible to hunt."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c03",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la majorité des cas, le tamarro est l'équivalent pyrénéen du fameux dahu, un quadrupède également indéterminé dont les seules caractéristiques connues sont d'avoir deux pattes plus courtes d'un côté que de l'autre",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Umumnya tamarro adalah padanan Pyrenees dari dahu yang terkenal, hewan berkaki empat yang juga tak jelas wujudnya, dengan satu-satunya ciri berupa dua kaki di satu sisi lebih pendek daripada sisi lain.",
        "en": "In most cases the tamarro is the Pyrenean equivalent of the famous dahu, a likewise undefined quadruped whose only known feature is having two legs shorter on one side than the other."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c04",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s1",
      "quote": "Le tamarro est un animal mythique dont il n'y a pas de description précise, laissant à chacun libre cours à son imagination.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tidak ada gambaran pasti tentang tamarro, sehingga setiap orang bebas membayangkannya.",
        "en": "There is no precise description of the tamarro, leaving everyone free to imagine it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c05",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s1",
      "quote": "Prétexte à l'amusement général, la chasse au tamarro, qui revêt un cérémonial très élaboré dans certaines régions de Catalogne, joue aussi un rôle de rituel initiatique",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Perburuan tamarro, yang di sebagian Katalonia memiliki upacara sangat rumit, menjadi hiburan bersama sekaligus ritual inisiasi.",
        "en": "The tamarro hunt, which has a very elaborate ceremony in parts of Catalonia, is general entertainment and also a rite of initiation."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c06",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s1",
      "quote": "La chasse se pratique de nuit, avec des bâtons, des pierres heurtées entre elles pour rabattre l'animal, des sacs pour le capturer",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Perburuan dilakukan pada malam hari dengan tongkat, batu yang diadu untuk menggiring hewan itu, dan karung untuk menangkapnya.",
        "en": "The hunt takes place at night, with sticks, stones knocked together to drive the animal, and sacks to catch it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c07",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s2",
      "quote": "A Andorra, la cacera del tamarro era una «batuda» falsa que s'organitzava per enganyar forasters ingenus als quals deien que es tractava d'una espècie rara, de pell molt apreciada.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Andorra, perburuan tamarro adalah perburuan palsu untuk menipu pendatang lugu yang diberi tahu bahwa tamarro spesies langka berkulit sangat berharga.",
        "en": "In Andorra the tamarro hunt was a fake beat organised to trick naive outsiders, who were told it was a rare species with a highly prized pelt."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c08",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s2",
      "quote": "existeix la figura del gambutzí, un ésser imaginari de característiques semblants que en altres zones de la península ibèrica es coneix com a gamusino, gamburrino o gambozino.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di wilayah Katalan lain ada gambutzí, makhluk khayalan serupa yang di bagian lain Semenanjung Iberia disebut gamusino, gamburrino, atau gambozino.",
        "en": "Elsewhere there is the gambutzí, a similar imaginary being known in other parts of the Iberian Peninsula as gamusino, gamburrino or gambozino."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c09",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s2",
      "quote": "L'any 1991, en Tamarro va ser la mascota dels Jocs dels Petits Estats d'Europa, amb seu a Andorra la Vella.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 1991, Tamarro menjadi maskot Pesta Olahraga Negara-Negara Kecil Eropa di Andorra la Vella.",
        "en": "In 1991 Tamarro was the mascot of the Games of the Small States of Europe held in Andorra la Vella."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c10",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s2",
      "quote": "l'any 2021 es va donar el nom de Tamarro insperatus a un dinosaure carnívor que es va descriure a partir de les restes fòssils trobades l'any 2003 en un jaciment del Pallars Jussà.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2021 nama Tamarro insperatus diberikan kepada dinosaurus karnivora yang dideskripsikan dari fosil yang ditemukan pada 2003 di Pallars Jussà.",
        "en": "In 2021 the name Tamarro insperatus was given to a carnivorous dinosaur described from fossils found in 2003 in the Pallars Jussà."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tamarro-c11",
      "source_id": "tamarro-s3",
      "quote": "Lo tamarro es un dels animals totemics de Vendargues.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Oksitan menyebut tamarro salah satu hewan totem kota Vendargues.",
        "en": "The Occitan article says the tamarro is one of the totem animals of Vendargues."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Tardo adalah duende jahat dari cerita rakyat Galisia dan Portugal yang masuk ke rumah pada malam hari dan duduk di dada orang tidur, menimbulkan mimpi buruk.",
    "en": "The tardo is a malevolent duende of Galician and Portuguese folklore that enters houses at night and sits on sleepers' chests, causing nightmares.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "tardo-c01",
      "tardo-c03",
      "tardo-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Tardo adalah duende mitologi Galisia, mirip trasno tetapi jauh lebih jahat; dalam cerita rakyat Portugal ia juga disebut pesadelo. Ia kecil, berbulu, bergigi banyak, kehijauan, berjanggut, bertopi merah, dan membawa pedang kecil. Ia tinggal di bawah tanah dan pada malam hari masuk ke rumah untuk duduk di dada orang tidur, menimbulkan sakit dan mimpi buruk.",
      "en": "The tardo is a duende of Galician mythology, like the trasno but far nastier; in Portuguese folklore it is also called pesadelo. It is small, hairy, many-toothed, greenish, bearded, red-capped and carries a small sword. It lives underground and at night gets into houses to sit on sleepers' chests, causing pain and nightmares.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "tardo-c01",
        "tardo-c02",
        "tardo-c03",
        "tardo-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia bisa berwujud anjing, kucing, atau kambing, dan di persimpangan membuat orang tersesat dengan mengencingi kaki mereka. Untuk mengusirnya orang menaruh mangkuk biji-bijian, karena tardo akan terus menghitungnya sampai pagi.",
      "en": "It can take the form of a dog, cat or goat, and at crossroads makes people lose their way by urinating on their legs. To be rid of it people leave a bowl of grain, which the tardo will keep counting until morning.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "tardo-c04",
        "tardo-c06",
        "tardo-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "tardo-c01",
      "source_id": "tardo-s1",
      "quote": "El tardo es un duende de la mitología gallega, similar al trasno pero mucho más malévolo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tardo adalah duende (peri rumah) mitologi Galisia, mirip trasno tetapi jauh lebih jahat.",
        "en": "The tardo is a duende (house sprite) of Galician mythology, like the trasno but far more malevolent."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tardo-c02",
      "source_id": "tardo-s1",
      "quote": "Es pequeño, peludo, con muchos dientes, de color verdoso y ojos redondos y negros; tiene barba y va vestido con ropas viejas y un gorro rojo. Además, se diferencia de otros duendes porque lleva una pequeña y afilada espada.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia kecil, berbulu, bergigi banyak, berwarna kehijauan dengan mata bulat hitam, berjanggut, berpakaian tua dan bertopi merah; berbeda dari duende lain, ia membawa pedang kecil yang tajam.",
        "en": "It is small, hairy and many-toothed, greenish with round black eyes, bearded, in old clothes and a red cap; unlike other duendes it carries a small sharp sword."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tardo-c03",
      "source_id": "tardo-s1",
      "quote": "Acostumbra a vivir bajo tierra y aprovecha la oscuridad de la noche para entrar en las casas, y así, sentarse sobre el pecho de las personas dormidas, provocándoles dolor y terribles pesadillas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia biasa tinggal di bawah tanah dan memanfaatkan gelap malam untuk masuk ke rumah lalu duduk di dada orang tidur, menimbulkan rasa sakit dan mimpi buruk.",
        "en": "It usually lives underground and uses the dark of night to get into houses and sit on sleepers' chests, causing pain and terrible nightmares."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tardo-c04",
      "source_id": "tardo-s1",
      "quote": "Para librarse de él basta con dejar un cuenco con granos de maíz o algún otro cereal. Como es muy curioso, irá a ver el cuenco y empezará a contar los granos, pero como sólo sabe contar hasta cien, volverá a empezar una y otra vez",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk mengusirnya cukup menaruh mangkuk berisi biji jagung atau serealia lain; karena sangat ingin tahu, ia akan menghitung bijinya, tetapi hanya bisa menghitung sampai seratus sehingga terus mengulang dari awal.",
        "en": "To be rid of it, simply leave a bowl of maize or other grain; being very curious it will count the grains, but since it can only count to a hundred it starts over and over."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tardo-c05",
      "source_id": "tardo-s2",
      "quote": "O Tardo ou Trevor é uma espécie de duende, um ser mítico do folclore popular português e galego. Ao tardo também se chama pesadelo ou tardo moleiro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Portugis, Tardo atau Trevor adalah sejenis duende dalam cerita rakyat Portugal dan Galisia, juga disebut pesadelo atau tardo moleiro.",
        "en": "According to the Portuguese article, the Tardo or Trevor is a kind of duende of Portuguese and Galician folklore, also called pesadelo or tardo moleiro."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tardo-c06",
      "source_id": "tardo-s2",
      "quote": "O tardo pode aparecer na figura de um animal e frequentemente aparece na figura de um cão, gato ou cabra.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tardo dapat muncul berwujud hewan, sering sebagai anjing, kucing, atau kambing.",
        "en": "The tardo can appear as an animal, often a dog, cat or goat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tardo-c07",
      "source_id": "tardo-s2",
      "quote": "O tardo quando aparece nos caminhos, nos regatos e nas encruzilhadas tenta deixar as pessoas intardadas (desorientadas) sem saber qual caminho seguir, urinando nas pernas das pessoas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di jalan, sungai kecil, dan persimpangan, tardo berusaha membuat orang tersesat dengan mengencingi kaki mereka.",
        "en": "On roads, by streams and at crossroads the tardo tries to make people lose their way by urinating on their legs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tardo-c08",
      "source_id": "tardo-s3",
      "quote": "Eliseo Alonsoren A rúa do noxo eleberrian, protagonistetako batek dio, mozkortuta dagoenean, berarekin batera bezatutako tardo bat duela.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Basque, dalam novel Eliseo Alonso A rúa do noxo seorang tokoh utama yang mabuk mengaku memelihara tardo jinak.",
        "en": "According to the Basque article, in Eliseo Alonso's novel A rúa do noxo a drunken protagonist claims to keep a tame tardo."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    "id": "Volkolak adalah manusia serigala dalam mitologi Slavia: penyihir yang sengaja menjadi serigala, orang yang terlahir atau dikutuk berubah wujud, dan korban sihir.",
    "en": "The volkolak is the werewolf of Slavic mythology: a sorcerer who becomes a wolf at will, a person born or cursed to shapeshift, or a victim of witchcraft.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dalam mitologi Slavia, manusia serigala atau volkolak adalah manusia yang untuk sementara berwujud serigala; ia tampak seperti serigala biasa, tetap berakal manusia, tetapi tidak dapat berbicara. Kepercayaan ini kuno dan dikenal di semua bangsa Slavia, paling rinci di Belarus, Polandia, dan Ukraina; di Rusia istilah volkolak hanya tercatat di selatan, dan Slavia Selatan mencampurnya dengan vampir.",
      "en": "In Slavic mythology the werewolf or volkolak is a person who temporarily becomes a wolf; it looks like an ordinary wolf and keeps a human mind but cannot speak. The belief is ancient and known to all Slavic peoples, most fully among Belarusians, Poles and Ukrainians; among Russians the word volkolak is recorded only in the south, and South Slavic beliefs merge it with the vampire.",

    {

      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "In Slavic mythology, a werewolf is a human-shapeshifter who temporarily takes the form of a wolf. Werewolves were often described as ordinary wolves, though some accounts noted peculiarities in appearance or behavior that hinted at their human origin. Werewolves retain human intelligence but cannot speak.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Slavia, manusia serigala adalah manusia pengubah wujud yang untuk sementara berwujud serigala; ia sering digambarkan seperti serigala biasa dengan beberapa keanehan yang menandakan asal manusianya, tetap berakal manusia tetapi tidak dapat berbicara.",
        "en": "In Slavic mythology a werewolf is a human shapeshifter who temporarily takes wolf form; often described as an ordinary wolf with some oddities betraying its human origin, it keeps human intelligence but cannot speak."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c02",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "The concept is ancient and appears to varying degrees among all Slavic peoples, with the most detailed accounts among Belarusians, Poles, and Ukrainians. In Russian folklore, the character is often simply called a shapeshifter, sharing clear similarities with the werewolf.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konsep ini kuno dan dikenal di semua bangsa Slavia, paling rinci di kalangan orang Belarus, Polandia, dan Ukraina; dalam cerita rakyat Rusia tokoh ini sering disebut pengubah wujud saja.",
        "en": "The idea is ancient and found among all Slavic peoples, most fully among Belarusians, Poles and Ukrainians; in Russian folklore the figure is often simply called a shapeshifter."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c03",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Among Russians, the term volkolak is recorded only in southern regions",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di kalangan orang Rusia, istilah volkolak hanya tercatat di wilayah selatan.",
        "en": "Among Russians the term volkolak is recorded only in southern regions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c04",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s3",
      "quote": "Ваўкалак, ваўкалака — чалавек, ператвораны ў ваўка, пярэварацень.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bahasa Belarus, vaŭkalak atau vaŭkalaka adalah manusia yang diubah menjadi serigala, sang pengubah wujud.",
        "en": "In Belarusian, the vaŭkalak or vaŭkalaka is a person turned into a wolf, a shapeshifter."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c05",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "It was believed that sorcerers could transform into wolves by reciting spells and performing actions such as leaping, stepping over, tumbling through, or passing through magically imbued objects, or draping them over themselves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyihir dipercaya dapat menjadi serigala dengan membaca mantra lalu melompati, melangkahi, berguling melewati, atau menerobos benda bertuah, atau menyampirkannya ke tubuh.",
        "en": "Sorcerers were believed to turn into wolves by reciting spells and leaping over, stepping over, tumbling through or passing through charmed objects, or draping them over themselves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c06",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "To revert to human form, sorcerers typically needed to repeat the actions in reverse. Sorcerers voluntarily became werewolves to cause harm to others.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk kembali menjadi manusia, penyihir mengulang tindakan itu secara terbalik; mereka menjadi manusia serigala dengan sengaja untuk mencelakai orang lain.",
        "en": "To turn back into humans, sorcerers repeated the actions in reverse; they became werewolves on purpose to harm others."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c07",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s3",
      "quote": "Ператварэнне па ўласнай волі адбываецца шляхам перакульвання праз уторкнутыя вастрыём уверх у зямлю нажы (іх колькасць у розных паданнях падаецца ад аднаго да дванаццаці — 1, 5, 7, 12).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kepercayaan Belarus, perubahan sukarela dilakukan dengan berjungkir balik melewati pisau yang ditancapkan ke tanah dengan ujung ke atas, berjumlah antara 1 dan 12 menurut versinya (1, 5, 7, 12).",
        "en": "In Belarusian belief, a willed change is made by somersaulting over knives stuck point-up in the ground, from one to twelve in different tales (1, 5, 7, 12)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c08",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Some beliefs described people born with a predisposition to periodic shapeshifting due to their parents' actions or as punishment for their own sins. Such werewolves were thought to exhibit zoomorphic traits in human form, such as hair resembling wolf fur.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian kepercayaan menyebut orang yang sejak lahir cenderung berubah wujud secara berkala karena perbuatan orang tuanya atau sebagai hukuman atas dosanya sendiri; dalam wujud manusia mereka punya ciri hewan seperti rambut mirip bulu serigala.",
        "en": "Some beliefs told of people born prone to periodic shapeshifting because of their parents' deeds or as punishment for their own sins; in human form they showed animal traits such as wolf-like hair."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c09",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "These werewolves were believed to lack control in wolf form, attacking livestock and even humans, including loved ones, and were sometimes associated with cannibalism. Ancient beliefs linked werewolves to celestial events like eclipses.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manusia serigala jenis ini dipercaya tidak dapat mengendalikan diri dalam wujud serigala, menyerang ternak bahkan manusia, termasuk orang terkasih, dan kadang dikaitkan dengan kanibalisme; kepercayaan kuno juga mengaitkannya dengan gerhana.",
        "en": "Such werewolves were thought to lose control in wolf form, attacking livestock and even people, loved ones included, and were sometimes tied to cannibalism; ancient beliefs also linked them with eclipses."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c10",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Folk beliefs also held that sorcerers or witches could transform a person into a wolf, often as an act of revenge, by casting spells on a wolf skin, belt, or enchanted door, among other methods. A popular narrative involved transforming an entire wedding party into wolves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyihir laki-laki atau perempuan juga dipercaya dapat mengubah seseorang menjadi serigala, sering sebagai balas dendam, dengan memantrai kulit serigala, sabuk, atau pintu; kisah populer menceritakan seluruh rombongan pernikahan diubah menjadi serigala.",
        "en": "Sorcerers or witches were also believed able to turn a person into a wolf, often in revenge, by bewitching a wolf skin, a belt or a door; a popular tale has a whole wedding party turned into wolves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c11",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "Involuntary werewolves suffered fear and despair, longing for human life and avoiding true wolves. They were thought to avoid carrion and raw meat, subsisting on foraged food or stolen human provisions.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Manusia serigala yang terpaksa menderita ketakutan dan putus asa, merindukan hidup sebagai manusia, menjauhi serigala sejati, dan menghindari bangkai serta daging mentah.",
        "en": "Involuntary werewolves suffered fear and despair, longed for human life, avoided real wolves and shunned carrion and raw meat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c12",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s1",
      "quote": "The myth likely originated from ancient totemic beliefs and rites of youthful initiation. The werewolf image may have been influenced by observations of people with physical or mental disorders or of old and sick wolves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mitos ini kemungkinan berasal dari kepercayaan totem kuno dan ritus inisiasi pemuda, dan citranya mungkin dipengaruhi pengamatan terhadap orang dengan gangguan fisik atau jiwa atau serigala tua dan sakit.",
        "en": "The myth probably grew from ancient totemic beliefs and youth initiation rites, and its image may reflect observations of people with physical or mental disorders or of old, sick wolves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-c13",
      "source_id": "werewolf-in-slavic-mythology-s2",
      "quote": "Южнославянские представления смешивают волколаков с упырями (вампирами).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Rusia, kepercayaan Slavia Selatan mencampuradukkan volkolak dengan upir (vampir).",
        "en": "According to the Russian article, South Slavic beliefs conflate volkolaks with upirs (vampires)."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    },

      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
    "en": "Zaldi, 'horse', is a horse-shaped spirit of Basque mythology, a form taken by witches and by Mari, including the headless white horse that foretells death.",

    {
      "id": "Zaldi, \"kuda\" dalam bahasa Basque, adalah irelu atau roh dalam legenda Basque. Kuda adalah salah satu wujud yang diambil para penyihir (sorginak) dan juga dewi Mari, dan sejumlah roh bawah tanah digambarkan berwujud kuda.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c01",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s1",
      "quote": "Zaldi est un Irelu ou génie présent dans les contes et légendes de la mythologie basque. Dans de nombreux contes mythiques, c'est l'une des figures adoptées par les sorcières ou Sorginak",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zaldi adalah irelu atau roh dalam dongeng dan legenda mitologi Basque, dan dalam banyak kisah merupakan salah satu wujud yang diambil para penyihir (sorginak).",
        "en": "Zaldi is an irelu or spirit of the tales and legends of Basque mythology, and in many tales one of the forms taken by witches (sorginak)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c02",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s1",
      "quote": "Zaldi signifie « cheval » en basque. Le suffixe a désigne l'article : zaldia se traduit donc par « le cheval ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zaldi berarti \"kuda\" dalam bahasa Basque; zaldia berarti \"si kuda\".",
        "en": "Zaldi means 'horse' in Basque; zaldia means 'the horse'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c03",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s3",
      "quote": "En molts contes i llegendes és una de les diverses formes que adopten les sorginak (les bruixes basques), però també Mari, la principal deessa basca.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, kuda adalah salah satu wujud yang diambil sorginak dan juga Mari, dewi utama Basque.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article, the horse is one of the forms taken by the sorginak and also by Mari, the chief Basque goddess."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c04",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s1",
      "quote": "En effet, c'est sous forme de cheval que sont représentés certains génies souterrains ou des personnages mis en scène dans des récits mythiques du peuple basque.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejumlah roh bawah tanah dan tokoh kisah mitos Basque digambarkan berwujud kuda.",
        "en": "Some underground spirits and characters of Basque myths are depicted in horse form."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c05",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la région de Tardets (Soule), de la caverne de Laxarrigibel, située dans un des contreforts d'Ahüski, près d'Alçay-Alçabéhéty-Sunharette, sort un être qui se manifeste sous les traits d'un cheval blanc. Il séquestra une fois un jeune homme de ce pays",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di daerah Tardets (Soule), dari gua Laxarrigibel di lereng Ahüski dekat Alçay-Alçabéhéty-Sunharette, keluar makhluk berwujud kuda putih yang pernah menyekap seorang pemuda setempat.",
        "en": "Near Tardets (Soule), from the Laxarrigibel cave on a spur of Ahüski near Alçay-Alçabéhéty-Sunharette, a being in the shape of a white horse comes out; it once held a local young man captive."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c06",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s1",
      "quote": "Zamari zuria, est un cheval blanc, un cheval sans tête qui hante les montagnes basques ... Le voir est un présage de mort ou, tout au moins, de malheur imminent.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zamari zuria adalah kuda putih tanpa kepala yang menghantui pegunungan Basque; melihatnya pertanda kematian atau setidaknya kemalangan yang segera datang.",
        "en": "Zamari zuria is a white, headless horse haunting the Basque mountains; seeing it portends death or at least imminent misfortune."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c07",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s1",
      "quote": "Le Zamari zuria, représentant l'esprit du maïs des céréales, est représenté par un danseur dans les fêtes de la Soule et du Guipuscoa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Zamari zuria, yang mewakili roh jagung dan serealia, diperankan seorang penari dalam pesta di Soule dan Gipuzkoa.",
        "en": "Zamari zuria, representing the spirit of maize and cereals, is played by a dancer at festivals in Soule and Gipuzkoa."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c08",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s1",
      "quote": "Un soir la domestique vit la jument noire et l'enfourcha, mais l'animal l'embarqua et la mena au gouffre d'Obantzun, bientôt, il n'y eut plus aucun signe de vie de la jeune femme.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda kuda betina Obantzun, seorang pelayan menunggangi kuda betina hitam yang ternyata membawanya ke jurang Obantzun, dan perempuan itu tak pernah terlihat lagi.",
        "en": "In the legend of the Obantzun mare, a servant girl mounts a black mare that carries her off to the Obantzun chasm, and she is never seen alive again."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c09",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s2",
      "quote": "En los monts de Sola y Ultrapuertos cuan amaneix lo caballo sin capeza gosa estar un anuncio de la muerte.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Aragon, di pegunungan Sola dan Ultrapuertos kemunculan kuda tanpa kepala biasanya menandakan kematian.",
        "en": "According to the Aragonese article, in the mountains of Sola and Ultrapuertos the appearance of the headless horse usually heralds death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "zaldi-c10",
      "source_id": "zaldi-s3",
      "quote": "A les típiques xiberoko maskaradak ('mascarades de Zuberoa'), pròpies del Carnaval, hi ha el personatge del Zamaltzain ('matalot, mosso de quadra'), que duu una indumentària tradicional, sovint anomenada \"cavall-falda\", que representa un genet amb el seu cavall.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam maskarade Karnaval Zuberoa ada tokoh Zamaltzain yang memakai kostum \"kuda-rok\" yang menggambarkan penunggang dan kudanya.",
        "en": "In the Carnival masquerades of Zuberoa there is the Zamaltzain, whose 'horse-skirt' costume shows a rider with his horse."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia fr, an, dan ca; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
    "id": "Abalam adalah raja neraka dalam demonologi, pembantu Paimon yang mengurus persembahan saat Paimon dipanggil.",
    "en": "Abalam is an infernal king of demonology, an assistant of Paimon who handles the offerings when Paimon is summoned.",

      "id": "Abalam, juga Abalim atau Abali, adalah salah satu raja neraka dalam demonologi dan pembantu Paimon. Ia mengurus persembahan minuman dan kurban dalam pemanggilan Paimon dan muncul bersama raja Bebal. Goetia menyebutnya raja kecil tanpa segel sendiri, sehingga ia hanya dapat dipanggil melalui tuannya.",
      "en": "Abalam, also Abalim or Abali, is one of the kings of hell in demonology and an assistant of Paimon. He handles libations and sacrifices when Paimon is summoned and appears with the king Bebal. The Goetia calls him a lesser king without a seal of his own, so he can only be summoned through his master.",

      "locator": null,

    {
      "id": "abalam-c02",
      "source_id": "abalam-s1",
      "quote": "Il suo compito consiste nell'occuparsi di libagioni e sacrifici che vengono offerti durante un'evocazione diretta solamente al suo superiore, comparendo in questo caso insieme al re infernale Bebal.",
      "locator": null,

      "source_id": "abalam-s1",

      "source_id": "abalam-s2",
      "quote": "Znany również pod imieniem Abalim. Goecja tytułuje go pomniejszym królem. Nie ma własnej pieczęci, jednakże można go wywołać przez pośrednictwo jego pana.",
```

```json
  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Amilamia adalah peri baik hati mitologi Basque dari Agurain, Alava, berambut emas yang tinggal di gua Lezao dan menolong orang miskin.",
    "en": "Amilamia is a kindly fairy of Basque mythology from Agurain, Alava, with golden hair, who lives in the Lezao cave and helps the poor.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "amilamia-c01",
      "amilamia-c02",
      "amilamia-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Amilamia adalah makhluk gaib baik hati mitologi Basque dari daerah Agurain di Alava, yang menurut tradisi tinggal di gua Lezao di pegunungan Entzia. Ia perempuan berambut pirang seperti emas yang menjuntai sampai tanah; ia menyisirnya dengan sisir emas di tepi kolam gua sambil bernyanyi dalam bahasa tak dikenal. Ia punya ayakan ajaib yang menghasilkan tepung walau kosong dan menolong orang miskin serta gembala.",
      "en": "Amilamia is a benevolent supernatural being of Basque mythology from the Agurain area of Alava who by tradition lived in the Lezao cave in the Entzia range. She is a woman with golden-blond hair reaching the ground, which she combs with a golden comb by the cave pool while singing in an unknown tongue. She had a magic sieve that made flour though empty, and helped the poor and shepherds.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "amilamia-c01",
        "amilamia-c02",
        "amilamia-c03",
        "amilamia-c04",
        "amilamia-c05",
        "amilamia-c10"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Federico Baraibar dan José Miguel de Barandiaran mencatat Amilamia sebagai nama lamia di Agurain, dan Pemerintah Basque menyebutnya salah satu nama daerah untuk lamiak. Ia varian lamiak yang lebih lembut; sebagian legenda menyebutnya satu tokoh, yang lain satu jenis roh.",
      "en": "Federico Baraibar and José Miguel de Barandiaran record Amilamia as the name of a lamia at Agurain, and the Basque Government lists it among regional names for the lamiak. She is a gentler variant of the lamiak; some legends make her one figure, others a kind of spirit.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "amilamia-c06",
        "amilamia-c07",
        "amilamia-c08",
        "amilamia-c09"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c01",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s1",
      "quote": "Amilamia (au pluriel amilamiak) est un être surnaturel bienveillant de la mythologie basque, particulièrement associé à la région d'Agurain, en Alava. Selon la tradition, elle aurait vécu dans la grotte de Lezao, dans le massif d'Entzia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amilamia (jamak: amilamiak) adalah makhluk gaib baik hati dalam mitologi Basque, terutama dikaitkan dengan daerah Agurain di Alava, dan menurut tradisi tinggal di gua Lezao di pegunungan Entzia.",
        "en": "Amilamia (plural amilamiak) is a benevolent supernatural being of Basque mythology, especially tied to the Agurain area of Alava, who by tradition lived in the Lezao cave in the Entzia range."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c02",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s1",
      "quote": "Elle est décrite comme une femme aux longs cheveux blonds, comparés à de l'or, qu'elle peigne avec un peigne en or en utilisant l'eau du bassin comme miroir. Sa chevelure est décrite comme suffisamment longue pour atteindre le sol.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan sebagai perempuan berambut pirang panjang seperti emas yang menyisir rambutnya dengan sisir emas sambil memakai air kolam sebagai cermin; rambutnya cukup panjang hingga menyentuh tanah.",
        "en": "She is described as a woman with long golden-blond hair, which she combs with a golden comb using the pool's water as a mirror; her hair is long enough to reach the ground."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c03",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s1",
      "quote": "Selon les récits associés à Amilamia, elle chantait également dans une langue inconnue pendant qu'elle se coiffait.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kisah-kisahnya, ia bernyanyi dalam bahasa yang tidak dikenal sambil menyisir rambut.",
        "en": "According to her tales she sang in an unknown language while combing her hair."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c04",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s1",
      "quote": "Elle aurait notamment possédé un tamis capable de produire de la farine alors qu'il était vide",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon memiliki ayakan yang dapat menghasilkan tepung walaupun kosong.",
        "en": "She is said to have owned a sieve that produced flour even when empty."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c05",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s1",
      "quote": "Elle est aussi présentée comme une figure bienveillante, venant en aide aux personnes pauvres et, dans certains récits, aux bergers.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan baik hati, menolong orang miskin dan, dalam beberapa kisah, para gembala.",
        "en": "She is presented as kindly, helping the poor and, in some tales, shepherds."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c06",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s1",
      "quote": "Dans la tradition recueillie par Federico Baraibar, le nom désigne une lamia de la région d'Agurain, associée à la grotte de Lezao, dans le massif d'Entzia. José Miguel de Barandiaran relève également Amilamia comme le nom donné à une lamia à Agurain.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tradisi yang dikumpulkan Federico Baraibar, nama ini merujuk lamia dari daerah Agurain yang terkait gua Lezao; José Miguel de Barandiaran juga mencatat Amilamia sebagai nama lamia di Agurain.",
        "en": "In the tradition collected by Federico Baraibar the name refers to a lamia of the Agurain area linked to the Lezao cave; José Miguel de Barandiaran also records Amilamia as the name of a lamia at Agurain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c07",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s1",
      "quote": "Le Gouvernement basque indique d'ailleurs que les lamiak sont désignées sous différentes appellations selon les régions, parmi lesquelles Amilamia et Eleilamia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemerintah Basque menyebut lamiak memiliki nama berbeda menurut daerah, antara lain Amilamia dan Eleilamia.",
        "en": "The Basque Government notes that lamiak have different names by region, among them Amilamia and Eleilamia."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c08",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s2",
      "quote": "Una amilamia és un geni de la mitologia basca, una variant suavitzada de les laminak o lamiak pròpia de la zona d'Agurain (Àlaba).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, amilamia adalah roh mitologi Basque, varian yang lebih lembut dari laminak atau lamiak dari daerah Agurain (Alava).",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article an amilamia is a spirit of Basque mythology, a gentler variant of the laminak or lamiak from the Agurain area (Alava)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c09",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s2",
      "quote": "Si bé algunes llegendes la descriuen com a un personatge singular, d'altres en parlen com a un tipus de geni al qual pertanyen nombrosos individus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian legenda menggambarkannya sebagai satu tokoh, sementara yang lain menyebutnya jenis roh dengan banyak anggota.",
        "en": "Some legends describe her as a single figure, others as a kind of spirit with many members."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amilamia-c10",
      "source_id": "amilamia-s3",
      "quote": "Amilamia euskal mitologiako irelu edo jeinua da, Agurain aldekoa. Entziako mendilerroko Lezao haitzuloan bizi omen zen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Basque menyebut Amilamia irelu atau roh mitologi Basque dari sekitar Agurain yang konon tinggal di gua Lezao di pegunungan Entzia.",
        "en": "The Basque article calls Amilamia an irelu or spirit of Basque mythology from around Agurain, said to live in the Lezao cave in the Entzia range."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Satu tokoh atau satu jenis",
        "en": "One figure or a kind"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Satu tokoh, Amilamia dari Lezao.",
            "en": "A single figure, Amilamia of Lezao."
          },
          "claim_ids": [
            "amilamia-c09"
          ]
        },
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Jenis roh dengan banyak anggota.",
            "en": "A kind of spirit with many members."
          },
          "claim_ids": [
            "amilamia-c09"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    }

}
```

```json
  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Butoni adalah momok penakut anak dalam cerita rakyat Valencia, monster buruk rupa bermuka dua yang datang membawa anak nakal tetapi tak pernah benar-benar tiba.",
    "en": "The Butoni is a child-scaring bogey of Valencian folklore, an ugly two-faced monster that comes for naughty children but never quite arrives.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "butoni-c01",
      "butoni-c02",
      "butoni-c03",
      "butoni-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Butoni, dengan nama daerah seperti Botoni, Bataroni, atau Batoni, adalah monster, roh, atau hantu dalam bestiari khayalan Valencia. Sejak Abad Pertengahan anak nakal diancam bahwa butoni akan datang membawa mereka bila menangis pada malam hari atau tidak menghabiskan makanan. Ia selalu datang tetapi tak pernah tiba; ia buruk rupa, kadang bermuka dua, bercakar, masuk lewat lubang kunci, dan bertanduk menyala.",
      "en": "The Butoni, with local names such as Botoni, Bataroni or Batoni, is a monster, spirit or ghost of the Valencian imaginary bestiary. Since the Middle Ages naughty children have been told the butoni will take them if they cry at night or leave their food. It is always coming but never arrives; it is ugly, sometimes two-faced and clawed, gets in through the keyhole and has a glowing horn.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "butoni-c01",
        "butoni-c02",
        "butoni-c03",
        "butoni-c04",
        "butoni-c05"
      ]

      "id": "Patroli kejam yang dibentuk jenderal Elío untuk memberantas bandit dijuluki \"ronda del Butoni\". Namanya mungkin dari bu dan Toni. Ia menjadi tokoh komik Butoni (2014) dan film pendek Espantacriaturas (2019).",
      "en": "The harsh patrol General Elío created against bandits was nicknamed the 'ronda del Butoni'. The name may come from bu plus Toni. It became the hero of the comic Butoni (2014) and the short film Espantacriaturas (2019).",

    {
      "id": "butoni-c01",
      "source_id": "butoni-s1",
      "quote": "El Butoni (Huerta de Valencia), Botoni (Safor), Bataroni (Ribera Alta y Ribera Baja), el Toni (Comtat), Butatoni (Campo de Murviedro) o Batoni (Tierra Alta) es un tipo de monstruo, espíritu, demonio, demonio menor, fantasma o fantasma terrorífico, personaje habitual en el bestiario del imaginario valenciano",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Butoni (Huerta de Valencia), Botoni (Safor), Bataroni (Ribera), Toni (Comtat), Butatoni (Campo de Murviedro), atau Batoni (Tierra Alta) adalah sejenis monster, roh, iblis kecil, atau hantu menakutkan yang lazim dalam bestiari khayalan Valencia.",
        "en": "The Butoni (Huerta de Valencia), Botoni (Safor), Bataroni (Ribera), Toni (Comtat), Butatoni (Campo de Murviedro) or Batoni (Tierra Alta) is a kind of monster, spirit, lesser demon or terrifying ghost common in the bestiary of Valencian imagination."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c02",
      "source_id": "butoni-s1",
      "quote": "Desde la Edad Media, en Valencia, se dice para acobardar los niños traviesos, con la advertencia de que si no se portaban bien, o lloraban por la noche, o no dormían o no se acababan la comida o la merienda, vendría el butoni y se los llevaría.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sejak Abad Pertengahan di Valencia, anak nakal ditakut-takuti dengan ancaman bahwa bila mereka tidak berkelakuan baik, menangis pada malam hari, tidak tidur, atau tidak menghabiskan makanan, butoni akan datang membawa mereka.",
        "en": "Since the Middle Ages in Valencia, naughty children have been warned that if they misbehaved, cried at night, would not sleep or did not finish their food, the butoni would come and take them away."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c03",
      "source_id": "butoni-s3",
      "quote": "En teoria, els butonis són uns éssers que apareixen quan els invoquen els grans, però de fet mai no acaben de fer-se visibles (sempre venen, però mai no arriben).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, butoni muncul ketika dipanggil orang dewasa tetapi tak pernah benar-benar terlihat: selalu datang, tetapi tak pernah tiba.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article, butonis appear when adults invoke them but never quite become visible: they are always coming but never arrive."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c04",
      "source_id": "butoni-s3",
      "quote": "Es tracta d'un personatge molt lleig representat de vegades amb dues cares i mans i peus amb urpes i que solia entrar pel badall o pel pany de la porta. Es deia que tenia una banya que s'il·luminava en la nit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia sangat buruk rupa, kadang digambarkan bermuka dua dengan tangan dan kaki bercakar, masuk lewat celah atau lubang kunci pintu, dan konon memiliki tanduk yang menyala pada malam hari.",
        "en": "It is very ugly, sometimes shown with two faces and clawed hands and feet, getting in through the door gap or keyhole, and was said to have a horn that glowed at night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c05",
      "source_id": "butoni-s2",
      "quote": "(существо наподобие Буки или Бабая в славянском фольклоре), которым в средние века пугали непослушных детей.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Rusia membandingkannya dengan Buka atau Babai dalam cerita rakyat Slavia, momok untuk menakuti anak nakal sejak Abad Pertengahan.",
        "en": "The Russian article compares it to the Buka or Babai of Slavic folklore, a bogey used to frighten naughty children since the Middle Ages."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c06",
      "source_id": "butoni-s1",
      "quote": "Un ejemplo histórico del uso de esta palabra como elemento atemoridor es la llamada \"ronda del Butoni\", nombre popular del cuerpo policial o patrulla de vigilancia creada en Valencia por el capitán general Elío después de la Guerra del Francés para combatir los bandoleros",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Contoh sejarah pemakaian nama ini untuk menakut-nakuti adalah \"ronda del Butoni\", julukan patroli yang dibentuk kapten jenderal Elío di Valencia setelah Perang Prancis untuk memberantas bandit.",
        "en": "A historical example of the name used to frighten is the 'ronda del Butoni', the nickname of the patrol set up in Valencia by Captain General Elío after the French War to fight bandits."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c07",
      "source_id": "butoni-s1",
      "quote": "Posiblemente de la unión de bu y el nombre propio de Toni.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya mungkin gabungan bu dan nama diri Toni.",
        "en": "The name possibly joins bu and the personal name Toni."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c08",
      "source_id": "butoni-s1",
      "quote": "El año 2014 el guionista Ricardo Vilbor y el dibujante Paco Zarco crearon un cómic llamado Butoni, basado en este personaje de la mitología valenciana.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2014 penulis Ricardo Vilbor dan ilustrator Paco Zarco membuat komik Butoni berdasarkan tokoh ini.",
        "en": "In 2014 the writer Ricardo Vilbor and the artist Paco Zarco created a comic called Butoni based on the figure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "butoni-c09",
      "source_id": "butoni-s1",
      "quote": "En el año 2019, el director valenciano Javier Guillot estrenaba su primer cortometraje Espantacriaturas",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2019 sutradara Valencia Javier Guillot merilis film pendek pertamanya, Espantacriaturas, yang dibintangi tokoh ini.",
        "en": "In 2019 the Valencian director Javier Guillot released his first short film, Espantacriaturas, starring the figure."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Caballucos del Diablu adalah kuda-kuda iblis bersayap capung dalam mitologi Cantabria, jiwa para pendosa yang muncul pada malam Santo Yohanes dengan amarah dan api.",
    "en": "The Caballucos del Diablu are dragonfly-winged devil horses of Cantabrian mythology, sinners' souls that burst out on St John's Eve in fury and fire.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "caballucos-del-diablu-c01",
      "caballucos-del-diablu-c02",

    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Caballucos del Diablu, \"kuda-kuda kecil iblis\", muncul pada malam Santo Yohanes (23 Juni) dari abu api unggun: kuda Percheron bersayap capung jarum, bersurai hitam, dan berbusa mulutnya. Mereka jiwa para pendosa yang dikutuk mengembara di Cantabria, tiap warna untuk dosa berbeda, misalnya kuda merah si lintah darat dan kuda kuning si hakim korup. Iblis sendiri menunggangi kuda merah yang menyemburkan api.",
      "en": "The Caballucos del Diablu, 'little horses of the devil', rise on St John's Eve (23 June) from the bonfire ashes: Percheron horses with damselfly wings, black manes and foaming mouths. They are sinners' souls doomed to roam Cantabria, each colour for a different sin, such as the red moneylender and the yellow corrupt judge. The Devil himself rides the fire-breathing red horse.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "caballucos-del-diablu-c01",
        "caballucos-del-diablu-c02",
        "caballucos-del-diablu-c03",
        "caballucos-del-diablu-c04",
        "caballucos-del-diablu-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Hentakan mereka meninggalkan jejak di batu, dan mereka menerkam siapa saja kecuali yang membawa verbena atau berada dekat api Santo Yohanes. Air liur mereka menjadi emas yang membuat kaya tetapi menyeret pengambilnya ke neraka. Artikel bahasa Spanyol dan Esperanto justru menggambarkan mereka sebagai tujuh capung besar.",
      "en": "Their stamping marks the rocks, and they pounce on anyone not carrying vervain or near St John's fire. Their saliva turns into gold that brings riches but drags the taker to hell. The Spanish and Esperanto articles instead describe them as seven big dragonflies.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c01",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "The Caballucos del Diablu (Cantabrian for \"(little) horses of the devil\") is a myth from the Cantabrian mythology, a region of northern Spain.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Caballucos del Diablu (bahasa Cantabria: \"kuda-kuda kecil iblis\") adalah mitos dari mitologi Cantabria di Spanyol utara.",
        "en": "The Caballucos del Diablu (Cantabrian for '(little) horses of the devil') is a myth of the mythology of Cantabria in northern Spain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c02",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "On St John's Eve (June 23) at night; when the people make bonfires to purify their souls, horses (Percheron purebred) with damselfly wings, black manes and foaming mouths appear amongst the ashes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada malam Santo Yohanes (23 Juni), ketika orang menyalakan api unggun untuk menyucikan jiwa, kuda-kuda Percheron bersayap capung jarum, bersurai hitam, dan berbusa mulutnya muncul dari abu.",
        "en": "On St John's Eve (23 June), when people light bonfires to cleanse their souls, Percheron horses with damselfly wings, black manes and foaming mouths rise among the ashes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c03",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "These stallions -the Caballucos- are the souls of sinners, damned to roam Cantabria for eternity, come to release their fury over a year's worth of sins, creating a rumbling explosion with fire accompanied by terrifying screams.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuda-kuda jantan ini adalah jiwa para pendosa yang dikutuk mengembara di Cantabria selamanya dan datang melampiaskan amarah dosa setahun dengan ledakan api bergemuruh dan jeritan mengerikan.",
        "en": "These stallions are the souls of sinners doomed to roam Cantabria forever, who come to vent a year of sins in a roaring burst of fire and terrifying screams."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c04",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "The red horse was a man who lent money to farmers and then used dirty tricks to steal their properties; the white one a miller who stole many thousands of dollars from his master; the black one a hermit who played tricks on people; the yellow one a corrupt judge",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kuda merah dahulu lintah darat yang menipu petani untuk merampas hartanya, kuda putih penggiling yang mencuri dari majikannya, kuda hitam pertapa penipu, dan kuda kuning hakim korup.",
        "en": "The red horse was a moneylender who tricked farmers out of their property, the white a miller who robbed his master, the black a deceiving hermit, and the yellow a corrupt judge."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c05",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "It is said that the Devil himself roams the streets riding the red fire-breathing steed, the sturdiest and most powerful who leads the raid, while other demons ride the rest.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon Iblis sendiri menunggangi kuda merah penyembur api, yang paling kuat dan memimpin rombongan, sementara iblis lain menunggangi sisanya.",
        "en": "The Devil himself is said to ride the red fire-breathing steed, the strongest, which leads the raid, while other demons ride the rest."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c06",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "The force in their stomping is such that their horseshoes leave prints on rocks, as if they were freshly ploughed soil.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hentakan mereka begitu kuat sehingga tapal kudanya meninggalkan jejak di batu seperti tanah yang baru dibajak.",
        "en": "Their stamping is so strong that their horseshoes leave prints on rocks as if on freshly ploughed soil."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c07",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "The Caballucos pounce on everyone they come across, the only things that repels them is a bunch of vervain that the person can carry along; the plant has to be collected the day before though, or should be placed next to St John’s fire, to which they won’t come near.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Caballucos menerkam siapa pun yang mereka temui; satu-satunya penangkalnya adalah seikat verbena yang dipetik sehari sebelumnya, atau diletakkan dekat api Santo Yohanes yang tidak akan mereka dekati.",
        "en": "The Caballucos pounce on anyone they meet; the only protection is a bunch of vervain picked the day before, or placed by St John's fire, which they will not approach."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c08",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s1",
      "quote": "The locals note that sometimes, after becoming worn out by the search, the Caballucos stop to rest and their saliva drips on the ground, and turns into gold ingots. Whoever takes them will be made extremely wealthy, but will descent straight to hell after death.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang, saat lelah dan beristirahat, air liur mereka menetes ke tanah dan menjadi batangan emas; siapa pun yang mengambilnya akan sangat kaya, tetapi langsung masuk neraka setelah mati.",
        "en": "Sometimes, tired and resting, their saliva drips to the ground and turns into gold ingots; whoever takes them becomes very rich but goes straight to hell after death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c09",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s2",
      "quote": "Según la tradición popular de Cantabria, los Caballucos del Diablu son siete seres con apariencia de grandes libélulas. Tienen alas largas y transparentes, y sobrevuelan los cielos durante la noche.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, dalam tradisi rakyat Cantabria Caballucos del Diablu adalah tujuh makhluk mirip capung besar bersayap panjang dan transparan yang terbang di langit malam.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, in Cantabrian folk tradition the Caballucos del Diablu are seven beings looking like big dragonflies, with long transparent wings, flying the night sky."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "caballucos-del-diablu-c10",
      "source_id": "caballucos-del-diablu-s3",
      "quote": "Ili estas sep grandaj libeloj aperantaj la nokton de la 23-a de junio.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Esperanto juga menyebut mereka tujuh capung besar yang muncul pada malam 23 Juni.",
        "en": "The Esperanto article also calls them seven big dragonflies appearing on the night of 23 June."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    "id": "Came-cruse atau camacrusa, \"kaki mentah\", adalah momok Gascogne berupa sebatang kaki, kadang bermata di lutut, yang muncul pada malam hari untuk membawa dan memakan orang yang lengah.",
    "en": "The came-cruse or camacrusa, 'raw leg', is a Gascon bogey in the form of a lone leg, sometimes with an eye on the knee, that comes at night to carry off and eat the careless.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "camacruda-c01",
      "camacruda-c02",
      "camacruda-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Came-cruse, dari bahasa Gascon cama crusa, \"kaki mentah\", adalah momok khas Gascogne dan Pyrenees. Ia sebatang kaki yang hidup sendiri, kadang bermata di lutut, dan muncul pada malam hari untuk membawa serta memakan orang yang lengah. Para narasumber menambahkan mulut, rambut, tanduk, atau cakar tanpa kesepakatan; di Ariège ia kaki bermata dan bertanduk yang berlari lebih cepat dari siapa pun.",
      "en": "The came-cruse, from Gascon cama crusa, 'raw leg', is a bogey of Gascony and the Pyrenees. It is a leg living on its own, sometimes with an eye on the knee, that comes at night to carry off and eat the careless. Informants add a mouth, hair, a horn or claws without agreeing; in Ariège it is a one-eyed horned leg that outruns everyone.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "camacruda-c01",
        "camacruda-c02",
        "camacruda-c03",
        "camacruda-c04",
        "camacruda-c07",
        "camacruda-c09",
        "camacruda-c10"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia bersembunyi di balik tumpukan jerami menurut Vincent Foix; di Gers dan Landes Abbé Dambielle menyebutnya samaran penyihir yang mirip vampir dan menculik anak kecil. Dua dongeng yang dikumpulkan Jean-François Bladé tentang kaki yang dicuri dari mayat mungkin menunjukkan asal-usulnya.",
      "en": "Vincent Foix says it hides behind haystacks; in Gers and the Landes Abbé Dambielle calls it a vampire-like sorcerer's disguise that snatches small children. Two tales collected by Jean-François Bladé about a leg stolen from a corpse may point to its origin.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "camacruda-c05",
        "camacruda-c06",
        "camacruda-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c01",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "La came-cruse est une créature fantastique propre à la Gascogne et aux régions avoisinantes (Pyrénées). C'est un croque-mitaine particulièrement effrayant.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Came-cruse adalah makhluk khayalan khas Gascogne dan daerah sekitarnya (Pyrenees), momok penakut anak yang sangat menyeramkan.",
        "en": "The came-cruse is a fantastic creature of Gascony and the nearby regions (the Pyrenees), an especially frightening bogeyman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c02",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "Elle est généralement décrite comme une jambe seule, parfois munie d'un œil au genou (la camba crusa dab l'uèlh dubèrt, la jambe crue avec l'œil ouvert). Elle surgit dans la nuit pour emporter, et dévorer, les imprudents.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia biasanya digambarkan sebagai sebatang kaki saja, kadang dengan mata di lutut (camba crusa dab l'uèlh dubèrt, \"kaki mentah dengan mata terbuka\"), yang muncul pada malam hari untuk membawa dan memakan orang yang lengah.",
        "en": "It is usually described as a lone leg, sometimes with an eye on the knee (camba crusa dab l'uèlh dubèrt, 'raw leg with the open eye'), which appears at night to carry off and devour the careless."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c03",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "Les « informateurs », comme les rares illustrateurs qui se sont essayés à une représentation, sont tentés de rajouter des éléments : bouche, cheveux, corne, griffes, etc., mais il n'y a pas d'unanimité dans leurs descriptions.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para narasumber dan ilustrator cenderung menambahkan mulut, rambut, tanduk, atau cakar, tetapi deskripsi mereka tidak seragam.",
        "en": "Informants and illustrators tend to add a mouth, hair, a horn or claws, but their descriptions do not agree."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c04",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "Du gascon cama crusa, « jambe crue ». ... Mais le sens littéral, « crue » au sens de « non-cuite », reste le plus plausible.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya dari bahasa Gascon cama crusa, \"kaki mentah\"; arti harfiah \"mentah\" (tidak dimasak) dianggap paling masuk akal.",
        "en": "The name comes from Gascon cama crusa, 'raw leg'; the literal sense of 'raw' (uncooked) is thought the most plausible."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c05",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "Selon l’abbé Vincent Foix, la came-crude se cache derrière les meules de paille ou de foin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Abbé Vincent Foix, came-crude bersembunyi di balik tumpukan jerami.",
        "en": "According to Abbé Vincent Foix, the came-crude hides behind haystacks and straw stacks."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c06",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "selon l'abbé Dambielle, la camo cruso serait une pèt (pèth, « peau » ou défroque, apparence que prendrait un sorcier comme celle du loup-garou). Elle évoque l'idée de vampire, et avec une férocité terrifiante rôde autour des maisons pour s'emparer des petits enfants.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Gers dan Landes, menurut Abbé Dambielle, camo cruso adalah pèt, wujud samaran penyihir seperti manusia serigala, mirip vampir, yang dengan buas mengintai rumah untuk menculik anak kecil.",
        "en": "In Gers and the Landes, according to Abbé Dambielle, the camo cruso is a pèt, a sorcerer's disguise like the werewolf's; vampire-like, it fiercely prowls round houses to seize small children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c07",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "«c'est une jambe avec un œil ouvert et une corne, qui court plus vite que tout le monde »",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Ariège ia digambarkan sebagai \"kaki dengan mata terbuka dan sebuah tanduk, yang berlari lebih cepat daripada siapa pun\".",
        "en": "In Ariège it is described as 'a leg with an open eye and a horn, which runs faster than anyone'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c08",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s1",
      "quote": "Deux contes de Gascogne recueillis par Jean-François Bladé, classés par Paul Delarue comme conte-type n° 366, peuvent permettre d'envisager une origine à cette croyance. Tous deux font référence à une jambe volée à un mort.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dua dongeng Gascogne yang dikumpulkan Jean-François Bladé, digolongkan Paul Delarue sebagai tipe dongeng 366, mungkin menunjukkan asal kepercayaan ini; keduanya bercerita tentang kaki yang dicuri dari mayat.",
        "en": "Two Gascon tales collected by Jean-François Bladé, classed by Paul Delarue as tale type 366, may hint at the belief's origin; both tell of a leg stolen from a corpse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c09",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s2",
      "quote": "La camacruda (del gascó cama cruda, 'cama crua'), també dita camacrusa, cambacrusa i d'altres maneres segons la zona, és una criatura fantàstica pròpia de la mitologia de Gascunya i les regions pirinenques circumdants.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Katalan menyebutnya camacruda, juga camacrusa atau cambacrusa, makhluk khayalan mitologi Gascogne dan daerah Pyrenees sekitarnya.",
        "en": "The Catalan article calls it camacruda, also camacrusa or cambacrusa, a fantastic creature of the mythology of Gascony and the surrounding Pyrenean regions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "camacruda-c10",
      "source_id": "camacruda-s3",
      "quote": "La camacrusa (o camacruda o cambacrusa) qu’ei ua creatura fantastica presenta en la Gasconha sancèra e dens las regions avesiadas (Pirenèus).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Oksitan juga menyebut camacrusa makhluk khayalan yang dikenal di seluruh Gascogne dan daerah Pyrenees sekitarnya.",
        "en": "The Occitan article also calls the camacrusa a fantastic creature found throughout Gascony and the neighbouring Pyrenean regions."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    {
      "id": "Draconcopedes, \"berkaki naga\", adalah binatang dalam kitab zoologi abad pertengahan. Vincent dari Beauvais menggambarkannya sebagai ular raksasa berkepala, berwajah, dan berdada perempuan, ular besar dan kuat yang berujung tubuh naga. Albertus Magnus menggolongkannya ular kelas ketiga dari genus naga berwajah seperti lelaki tak berjanggut, sehingga gigitannya lemah dan bisanya tidak mematikan.",
      "en": "The draconcopedes, 'dragon-footed', is a beast of the medieval zoologies. Vincent of Beauvais describes a giant serpent with a woman's head, face and breasts, great and powerful and ending in a dragon's body. Albertus Magnus places it among third-class serpents of the dragon genus with a face like an unbearded man's, so its bite is weak and its venom not deadly.",

      "quote": "The draconcopedes are what the Greeks call a large serpent of the third class and of the dragon genus which, they say, has the maidenly face of an unbearded man.",

      "statement": {

    },
    {
      "id": "draconcopedes-c07",
      "source_id": "draconcopedes-s2",
      "quote": "La fée Mélusine, avec son corps serpentin, est d'après Claude Lecouteux et Édouard Brasey le dracontopode le plus connu.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Claude Lecouteux dan Édouard Brasey, peri Mélusine yang bertubuh ular adalah dracontopode paling terkenal.",
        "en": "According to Claude Lecouteux and Édouard Brasey, the fairy Mélusine with her serpent body is the best-known dracontopode."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "draconcopedes-c08",
      "source_id": "draconcopedes-s2",
      "quote": "L'une de ces figures orne une décoration d'église romane à Lurcy-Lévis.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salah satu figur semacam ini menghiasi gereja Romanesk di Lurcy-Lévis.",
        "en": "One such figure adorns a Romanesque church decoration at Lurcy-Lévis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "draconcopedes-c09",
      "source_id": "draconcopedes-s3",
      "quote": "Con il termine dracontopode o draconcopode (dal latino dracontopedis, draconcopedis o draconcopodis, letteralmente \"dai piedi di drago\") si indicava, in epoca medievale, un essere leggendario dal volto umano e dal corpo di serpente",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bahasa Italia disebut dracontopode atau draconcopode, dari bahasa Latin dracontopedis, draconcopedis, atau draconcopodis, harfiah \"berkaki naga\", untuk makhluk legendaris abad pertengahan berwajah manusia dan bertubuh ular.",
        "en": "In Italian it is the dracontopode or draconcopode, from Latin dracontopedis, draconcopedis or draconcopodis, literally 'dragon-footed', a medieval legendary being with a human face and a serpent's body."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "draconcopedes-c10",
      "source_id": "draconcopedes-s3",
      "quote": "Nell'arte medievale e rinascimentale, Satana, nella forma del serpente tentatore del peccato originale, è spesso rappresentato come un dracontopode dai caratteri femminili.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam seni abad pertengahan dan Renaisans, Iblis sebagai ular penggoda dosa asal sering digambarkan sebagai dracontopode berciri perempuan.",
        "en": "In medieval and Renaissance art, Satan as the tempting serpent of original sin is often shown as a dracontopode with female features."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "draconcopedes-c11",
      "source_id": "draconcopedes-s3",
      "quote": "L'appartenenza del dracontopode al terzo ordine dei serpenti indica che il suo morso è debole e il suo veleno non è mortale",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena termasuk ular golongan ketiga, gigitannya lemah dan bisanya tidak mematikan.",
        "en": "Belonging to the third order of serpents means its bite is weak and its venom not deadly."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia en, fr, dan it; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lou Drapé adalah kuda hantu dari Aigues-Mortes, Prancis selatan, yang pada malam hari mengumpulkan anak-anak tersesat di punggungnya yang terus memanjang lalu membawa mereka pergi selamanya.",
    "en": "Lou Drapé is a ghostly horse of Aigues-Mortes in southern France that gathers lost children at night on its ever-lengthening back and carries them off for good.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lou Drapé adalah kuda legendaris Aigues-Mortes di rawa Petite Camargue. Pada malam hari ia berkeliaran di sekitar tembok kota dan mengumpulkan anak-anak tersesat di punggungnya, yang memanjang hingga muat lima puluh sampai seratus anak; anak-anak itu tidak pernah kembali. Kuda pucat ini lambang kematian dan dipakai untuk menakuti anak, mungkin versi drac Occitania yang dapat berwujud kuda.",
      "en": "Lou Drapé is a legendary horse of Aigues-Mortes in the Petite Camargue marshes. At night it roams the town walls and gathers lost children on its back, which stretches to hold fifty or a hundred; the children never return. This pale horse is a symbol of death used to frighten children, perhaps a version of the Occitan drac that can take horse form.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "drape-legend-c01",
        "drape-legend-c02",
        "drape-legend-c03",
        "drape-legend-c07"

    {
      "id": "Collin de Plancy mencatatnya pada 1818, dan pada 1965 anak-anak masih ditakut-takuti dengannya. Menurut Frédéric Mistral drapet berarti drac kecil, tetapi asal nama Lou Drapé tidak diketahui pasti. Konon anak terakhir yang menungganginya membebaskan semua anak dengan menyerukan Yesus, Maria, dan Santo Yosef. Ia termasuk kelompok kuda penculik yang terkait air, seperti drac Pyrenees.",
      "en": "Collin de Plancy recorded it in 1818, and in 1965 children were still threatened with it. Frédéric Mistral says drapet means a small drac, but the name's origin is uncertain.
```
