```json
      "published": null,
      "language": "eu",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "marra-c01",
      "source_id": "marra-s1",
      "quote": "Ahari ou Aari est un « bélier » dans la mythologie basque et un des génies nocturnes subalternes de Mari.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ahari atau Aari adalah \"domba jantan\" dalam mitologi Basque, salah satu roh malam bawahan Mari.",
        "en": "Ahari or Aari is a 'ram' in Basque mythology, one of Mari's subordinate night spirits."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marra-c02",
      "source_id": "marra-s1",
      "quote": "Ahari signifie « bélier » en basque.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ahari berarti \"domba jantan\" dalam bahasa Basque.",
        "en": "Ahari means 'ram' in Basque."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marra-c03",
      "source_id": "marra-s2",
      "quote": "Ahari és un dels ireluak, genis nocturns de la mitologia basca, que de vegades s'apareix com una dona o un nen, de vegades com un animal, i que viu a la famosa cova d'Okina, a Bernedo (Àlaba), en forma de marrà.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, Ahari adalah salah satu irelu, roh malam Basque, yang kadang muncul sebagai perempuan atau anak, kadang sebagai hewan, dan tinggal di gua Okina di Bernedo (Alava) dalam wujud domba jantan.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article, Ahari is one of the ireluak, Basque night spirits, which sometimes appears as a woman or child, sometimes as an animal, and lives in the Okina cave at Bernedo (Alava) as a ram."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marra-c04",
      "source_id": "marra-s2",
      "quote": "Generalment, els marrans \"irelu\" que viuen a les coves són servents de Mari. La deessa en fa servir el cos de coixí per a dormir i les banyes de filosa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Domba jantan irelu yang tinggal di gua umumnya pelayan Mari; sang dewi memakai tubuhnya sebagai bantal tidur dan tanduknya sebagai alat pintal.",
        "en": "The irelu rams living in caves are generally Mari's servants; the goddess uses their bodies as pillows and their horns as distaffs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marra-c05",
      "source_id": "marra-s2",
      "quote": "De vegades, quan surt de la cova, Mari fa servir els marrans de muntura, cavalcant-hi com si anés a cavall. Aquesta creença, per exemple, s'aplica al cas de la Dama d'Aketegi (un dels molts noms de Mari), a Zegama.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang ketika keluar dari gua Mari menunggangi domba jantan seperti kuda, misalnya dalam kisah Dama d'Aketegi, salah satu nama Mari, di Zegama.",
        "en": "Sometimes, leaving her cave, Mari rides the rams like horses, as in the case of the Lady of Aketegi, one of Mari's names, at Zegama."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marra-c06",
      "source_id": "marra-s2",
      "quote": "El marrà (en basc: ahari, amb les variants dialectals aari, adari, ari i altres) és un animal molt habitual en la mitologia basca",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Domba jantan (ahari, dengan varian dialek aari, adari, ari) adalah hewan yang sangat umum dalam mitologi Basque.",
        "en": "The ram (ahari, with dialect forms aari, adari, ari) is a very common animal in Basque mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marra-c07",
      "source_id": "marra-s1",
      "quote": "Dans les sports ruraux ou herri kirolak, parmi les nombreuses épreuves pastorales, on trouve le combat de béliers (ahari topeka)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam olahraga pedesaan Basque (herri kirolak) ada adu domba jantan (ahari topeka).",
        "en": "Basque rural sports (herri kirolak) include ram fighting (ahari topeka)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marra-c08",
      "source_id": "marra-s3",
      "quote": "Ahari Arabako Okinako leize ospetsuan bizi omen zen ireluetako baten izena da, ahariaren itxura hartzen zuena.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Basque juga menyebut Ahari nama irelu yang konon tinggal di gua Okina di Alava dan berwujud domba jantan.",
        "en": "The Basque article also says Ahari is the name of an irelu said to live in the famous Okina cave in Alava, taking the form of a ram."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    {
      "id": "Matlalcueye atau Matlalcueitl, \"yang berrok biru\", adalah dewi hujan, air di bumi, dan nyanyian dalam mitologi Tlaxcala. Ia permaisuri dewa hujan Tlaloc, istri keduanya setelah Xochiquetzal, dan juga disebut Chalchiuhtlicue, \"roknya dari giok\". Gunung api La Malinche atau Malintzin, yang dari kakinya mengalir sungai, memakai namanya.",
      "en": "Matlalcueye or Matlalcueitl, 'she of the blue skirt', is the goddess of rain, earthly waters and song of Tlaxcala. She is the consort of the rain god Tlaloc, his second wife after Xochiquetzal, and is also called Chalchiuhtlicue, 'her skirt of jade'. The volcano La Malinche or Malintzin, from whose slopes rivers flow, bears her name.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c01",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s1",
      "quote": "Matlalcueitl est la déesse de la pluie et des chansons dans la mythologie Tlaxcala. Elle a donné son nom à un volcan plus communément appelé La Malinche.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Matlalcueitl adalah dewi hujan dan nyanyian dalam mitologi Tlaxcala, yang memberi nama pada gunung api yang lebih dikenal sebagai La Malinche.",
        "en": "Matlalcueitl is the goddess of rain and songs in Tlaxcala mythology, who gave her name to a volcano better known as La Malinche."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c02",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s2",
      "quote": "es la Diosa de la lluvia y las aguas terrestres, consorte de Tlaloc, Dios de la lluvia, según los tlaxcaltecas. También fue conocida como Chalchiuhtlicue.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut orang Tlaxcala, Matlalcueye adalah dewi hujan dan air di bumi, permaisuri Tlaloc sang dewa hujan; ia juga dikenal sebagai Chalchiuhtlicue.",
        "en": "According to the Tlaxcalans, Matlalcueye is the goddess of rain and earthly waters, consort of Tlaloc the rain god; she was also known as Chalchiuhtlicue."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c03",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s2",
      "quote": "Su nombre quiere decir textualmente \"la de la falda azul\", por su color evidente y sus laderas largas. Su otro nombre, Chalchiuhtlicue, quiere decir \"su falda (de) jade\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya berarti \"yang berrok biru\", sesuai warna dan lereng panjang gunungnya; nama lainnya, Chalchiuhtlicue, berarti \"roknya dari giok\".",
        "en": "Her name literally means 'she of the blue skirt', from the mountain's colour and long slopes; her other name, Chalchiuhtlicue, means 'her skirt of jade'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c04",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s2",
      "quote": "Después de Xochiquetzal , es la segunda esposa de Tláloc: Malintzin, montaña de cuya falda efectivamente brotan ríos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Setelah Xochiquetzal, ia istri kedua Tlaloc: Malintzin, gunung yang dari kakinya memang mengalir sungai-sungai.",
        "en": "After Xochiquetzal she is Tlaloc's second wife: Malintzin, the mountain from whose skirts rivers truly flow."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c05",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s2",
      "quote": "También como otras montañas dadoras de lluvia, era llamada Coatlicue (la de la falda de víboras) pues las nubes eran comparadas con serpientes y decían que las serpientes de nubes (mixkoatl) nacían en la montaña",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seperti gunung pemberi hujan lain, ia juga disebut Coatlicue (\"yang berrok ular\"), karena awan diumpamakan ular dan ular awan (mixkoatl) dikatakan lahir di gunung itu.",
        "en": "Like other rain-giving mountains she was also called Coatlicue ('she of the serpent skirt'), since clouds were likened to snakes and cloud serpents (mixkoatl) were said to be born on the mountain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c06",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s2",
      "quote": "En lengua yumhu se llama Mhu Ye (Diosa de la lluvia) y como los pueblos nahuahablantes, la representan como una mujer mitad humana y mitad culebra y se narra que se aparece de esa forma a los pobladores tlaxcaltecas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam bahasa Yumhu (Otomi) ia disebut Mhu Ye, \"Dewi Hujan\", dan seperti bangsa berbahasa Nahuatl mereka menggambarkannya sebagai perempuan setengah manusia setengah ular yang menampakkan diri kepada penduduk Tlaxcala.",
        "en": "In the Yumhu (Otomi) language she is Mhu Ye, 'Rain Goddess', and like the Nahuatl-speaking peoples they depict her as half woman, half snake, appearing so to the people of Tlaxcala."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c07",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s3",
      "quote": "Matlalcueitl o Matlalcueyetl o Matlalcueje (in lingua náhuatl Matlaltic-Cueitl ovvero [Colei che Indossa una] Gonna Azzurra) è una divinità della pioggia donatrice di vita e della canzone, nella mitologia di Tlaxcala.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Italia menyebut Matlalcueitl atau Matlalcueyetl, \"yang memakai rok biru\", dewi hujan pemberi kehidupan dan nyanyian dalam mitologi Tlaxcala.",
        "en": "The Italian article calls Matlalcueitl or Matlalcueyetl, 'she who wears a blue skirt', a life-giving goddess of rain and song in Tlaxcala mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "matlalcueye-c08",
      "source_id": "matlalcueye-s3",
      "quote": "Nella mitologia azteca veniva identificata con Chalchiuhtlicue e con il consorte, l'antico dio della pioggia Tlaloc.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Aztek ia disamakan dengan Chalchiuhtlicue, permaisuri dewa hujan kuno Tlaloc.",
        "en": "In Aztec mythology she was identified with Chalchiuhtlicue, consort of the ancient rain god Tlaloc."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mazapégul adalah peri malam usil dari Romagna, Italia, bertopi merah dan berbulu kelabu, yang menimbulkan mimpi buruk, mengepang surai kuda, dan menyukai gadis cantik.",
    "en": "The mazapégul are mischievous night elves of Romagna, Italy, red-capped and grey-furred, who cause nightmares, braid horses' manes and dote on pretty girls.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "mazapegul-c01",
      "mazapegul-c02",
      "mazapegul-c03",
      "mazapegul-c04",
      "mazapegul-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mazapégul adalah peri malam usil dari cerita rakyat Romagna, terutama di Apennine Forlì. Mereka campuran kera dan manusia kecil berbulu kelabu yang memakai topi merah, sumber kekuatan usil mereka. Mereka menimbulkan mimpi buruk dan rasa tercekik, menghilangkan benda, membuat piring beterbangan, dan mengepang surai kuda; mereka juga tidur di atas perut gadis cantik sambil memujinya.",
      "en": "The mazapégul are mischievous night elves of the folklore of Romagna, above all the Forlì Apennines. They are grey-furred crosses of ape and homunculus wearing a red cap, the source of their mischief. They cause nightmares and choking, make things vanish, send dishes flying and braid horses' manes; they also sleep on pretty girls' stomachs and flatter them.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mazapegul-c01",
        "mazapegul-c02",
        "mazapegul-c03",
        "mazapegul-c04",
        "mazapegul-c05",
        "mazapegul-c11"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mencuri topinya atau menaburkan beras di jendela dapat menangkal mereka. Mereka diturunkan dari inkubus dan mirip Laurieddhu dari Italia selatan. Cerita tentang mereka dicatat Giuliano Fantaguzzi di Cesena awal abad ke-15, namanya muncul dalam kontrak tahun 1487 di Forlì, dan topi merahnya kini menjadi kostum karnaval Romagna.",
      "en": "Stealing its cap or scattering rice on the sill wards them off. They derive from the incubus and resemble the Laurieddhu of southern Italy. Giuliano Fantaguzzi recorded their lore in early 15th-century Cesena, the name appears in a 1487 contract at Forlì, and the red cap is now a Romagnol carnival costume.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c01",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "The mazapégul, also known by several other names in Romagnol, are mischievous nocturnal elves in the folklore of Romagna, especially around the Apennines of Forlì, in northern Italy.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mazapégul, yang punya banyak nama lain dalam bahasa Romagnol, adalah peri malam yang usil dalam cerita rakyat Romagna, terutama di Pegunungan Apennine sekitar Forlì, Italia utara.",
        "en": "The mazapégul, known by several other Romagnol names, are mischievous night elves of the folklore of Romagna, especially around the Apennines of Forlì in northern Italy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c02",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "The elves are portrayed as a cross between apes and homunculi, with grey fur, also resembling cats or monkeys. They wear a distinctive red cap, which they require to perform their mischief.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka digambarkan sebagai campuran kera dan manusia kecil berbulu kelabu, mirip kucing atau monyet, dan memakai topi merah khas yang mereka perlukan untuk berbuat usil.",
        "en": "They are shown as a cross between apes and homunculi with grey fur, also like cats or monkeys, wearing a distinctive red cap they need for their mischief."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c03",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "The mazapégul are credited for causing nightmares, including sleep paralysis or sensations of suffocation, ruffling women's hair in their sleep, causing stomach pains, making objects disappear, playing the cymbals, causing dishes to fly, and making victims gain weight.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mazapégul dianggap menyebabkan mimpi buruk, termasuk kelumpuhan tidur dan rasa tercekik, mengacak rambut perempuan yang tidur, sakit perut, menghilangkan benda, memainkan simbal, dan membuat piring beterbangan.",
        "en": "The mazapégul are blamed for nightmares, including sleep paralysis and choking sensations, tousling sleeping women's hair, stomach aches, vanishing objects, clashing cymbals and flying dishes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c04",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "In the countryside, they disturb animals, especially horses, who wake in the mornings covered in sweat and with braided tails and manes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di pedesaan mereka mengganggu hewan, terutama kuda, yang bangun pagi dengan tubuh berkeringat serta ekor dan surai terkepang.",
        "en": "In the countryside they bother animals, especially horses, which wake sweating with braided tails and manes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c05",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "Attracted by beautiful young girls, the elves enter their rooms at night and fall asleep on their stomachs. They compliment them, saying: \"What beautiful eyes! What beautiful hair!\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tertarik pada gadis cantik, mereka masuk ke kamar gadis pada malam hari, tidur di atas perutnya, dan memujinya: \"Betapa indah matamu! Betapa indah rambutmu!\"",
        "en": "Drawn to pretty girls, they enter their rooms at night, fall asleep on their stomachs and praise them: 'What beautiful eyes! What beautiful hair!'"
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c06",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "If its cap is stolen or thrown into the well, either its vanquisher is tormented at night with the demand of \"give me back my cap!\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bila topinya dicuri atau dilempar ke sumur, si pencuri bisa diganggu setiap malam dengan tuntutan \"kembalikan topiku!\".",
        "en": "If its cap is stolen or thrown down the well, the thief may be pestered at night with the cry 'give me back my cap!'"
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c07",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "sprinkling grains of rice on a windowsill so that the mazapégul is distracted by counting them until daybreak.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menaburkan butir beras di ambang jendela membuat mazapégul sibuk menghitungnya sampai fajar.",
        "en": "Grains of rice scattered on a windowsill keep the mazapégul busy counting them until daybreak."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c08",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "The mazapégul  descend from the mythological incubus, a demon that would disturb sleeping humans and take advantage of women. They share characteristics with elves from other regions of Italy, such as the Laurieddhu ... of southern Italy, as well as from the French Vosges.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mazapégul diturunkan dari inkubus, iblis yang mengganggu orang tidur dan memperdaya perempuan, dan memiliki kemiripan dengan peri daerah lain seperti Laurieddhu dari Italia selatan dan peri Vosges di Prancis.",
        "en": "The mazapégul descend from the incubus, a demon that troubled sleepers and preyed on women, and share traits with elves elsewhere, such as the Laurieddhu of southern Italy and those of the French Vosges."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c09",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "Folklore surrounding the mazapégul is recorded in a survey of early 15th-century Cesena by Giuliano Fantaguzzi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cerita rakyat tentang mazapégul tercatat dalam tinjauan Giuliano Fantaguzzi tentang Cesena awal abad ke-15.",
        "en": "Mazapégul lore is recorded in Giuliano Fantaguzzi's survey of early 15th-century Cesena."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c10",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s2",
      "quote": "El nombre de la familia que se menciona está documentado por primera vez en un extracto de un contrato de venta del 9 de mayo de 1487 en el Archivo general de Forlì",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, nama keluarga peri ini pertama kali tercatat dalam kutipan kontrak jual beli tertanggal 9 Mei 1487 di Arsip Umum Forlì.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, the family name is first recorded in an extract of a sales contract of 9 May 1487 in the General Archive of Forlì."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c11",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s3",
      "quote": "Il Mazapégul è un folletto della mitologia italiana, appartenente alla tradizione folclorica della Romagna, in particolare dell'Appennino forlivese.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Italia juga menyebut Mazapégul peri usil (folletto) dari tradisi rakyat Romagna, terutama Apennine Forlì.",
        "en": "The Italian article also calls the Mazapégul a sprite (folletto) of the folk tradition of Romagna, especially the Forlì Apennines."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mazapegul-c12",
      "source_id": "mazapegul-s1",
      "quote": "The mazapégul, particularly its red cap, features in Romagnol carnival costumes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mazapégul, terutama topi merahnya, muncul dalam kostum karnaval Romagna.",
        "en": "The mazapégul, especially its red cap, appears in Romagnol carnival costumes."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
    "id": "Mikelats adalah putra jahat dewi Mari dalam mitologi Basque, pencipta badai dan hujan es yang menghancurkan ladang, saudara Atarrabi yang baik.",
    "en": "Mikelats is the wicked son of the goddess Mari in Basque mythology, maker of storms and hail that ruin the fields, brother of the good Atarrabi.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mikelats adalah putra Mari dan Maju (Sugaar) dalam mitologi Basque, personifikasi kejahatan yang berlawanan dengan kakaknya Atarrabi. Ia menciptakan badai, melempar batu, dan menghantam ladang dengan hujan es, kadang bersama Mari untuk menghukum manusia yang tidak menghormati ibu mereka; kekuatannya disamakan dengan Thor. Dalam sebagian legenda ia disebut Hodei atau Odei, \"awan\", dan namanya gabungan Mikel dan hats, \"napas\".",
      "en": "Mikelats is the son of Mari and Maju (Sugaar) in Basque mythology, evil personified and opposite of his elder brother Atarrabi. He makes storms, hurls stones and strikes fields with hail, sometimes with Mari to punish people who disrespect their mother; his powers are likened to Thor's. Some legends call him Hodei or Odei, 'cloud', and his name joins Mikel and hats, 'breath'.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "mikelats-c02",
        "mikelats-c03",
        "mikelats-c04",
        "mikelats-c05"

    {
      "id": "Ia dan Atarrabi belajar di gua iblis dengan syarat salah satu harus tinggal; undian jatuh pada Mikelats, tetapi Atarrabi menggantikannya. Barandiaran mencatat kisah-kisah tentangnya sudah tercerai-berai di antara berbagai tokoh.",
      "en": "He and Atarrabi studied in the devil's cave on condition that one would stay; the lot fell to Mikelats, but Atarrabi took his place. Barandiaran noted that his stories are scattered among various figures.",

      "source_id": "mikelats-s1",
      "quote": "Mikelats (prononcer « mikélatche ») est un personnage de la mythologie basque, il crée les orages et projette des pierres. Il a des pouvoirs similaires au dieu Thor de la mythologie scandinave.",
      "locator": null,

        "id": "Mikelats adalah tokoh mitologi Basque yang menciptakan badai dan melemparkan batu, dengan kekuatan mirip dewa Thor dari mitologi Skandinavia.",

      "id": "mikelats-c02",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s1",
      "quote": "Mikelats est l'enfant de Mari et Maju. Les paysans le voient comme une créature maléfique. Selon une légende, Avec sa mère Mari, il crée des tempêtes et les lance contre le troupeau et la moisson, pour punir les humains qui ne respectaient pas leur mère.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mikelats adalah anak Mari dan Maju; para petani menganggapnya jahat, dan menurut legenda bersama ibunya ia menciptakan badai dan melemparkannya ke ternak dan panen untuk menghukum manusia yang tidak menghormati ibu mereka.",
        "en": "Mikelats is the child of Mari and Maju; farmers see him as evil, and by legend he and his mother make storms and hurl them at herds and harvests to punish people who did not respect their mother."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mikelats-c03",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s2",
      "quote": "Mikelats és un personatge destacat de la mitologia basca. És un dels dos fills de Mari (la deessa suprema basca) i Sugaar i representa la personificació del mal, mentre que el seu germà gran, Atarrabi, encarna el bé.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Katalan, ia salah satu dari dua putra Mari dan Sugaar yang mempersonifikasikan kejahatan, sedangkan kakaknya Atarrabi mewujudkan kebaikan.",
        "en": "According to the Catalan article, he is one of the two sons of Mari and Sugaar and personifies evil, while his elder brother Atarrabi embodies good."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mikelats-c04",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s2",
      "quote": "Mikelats, que també apareix com a Hodei o Odei ('núvol') en algunes llegendes, és considerat per tots els estudiosos com la personificació del mal, ja que posseeix el poder de colpejar camps i horts amb calamarsa per mitjà de tempestes",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sebagian legenda ia muncul sebagai Hodei atau Odei (\"awan\"), dan para peneliti menganggapnya personifikasi kejahatan karena dapat menghantam ladang dan kebun dengan hujan es lewat badai.",
        "en": "In some legends he appears as Hodei or Odei ('cloud'), and scholars see him as evil personified because he can strike fields and orchards with hail through storms."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mikelats-c05",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s2",
      "quote": "El seu nom ja és prou indicatiu, car està format per Mikel (Miquel, el nom de l'arcàngel principal, vencedor de Llucifer) i hats ('alè').",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya terdiri dari Mikel (Mikael, nama malaikat agung pengalah Lucifer) dan hats (\"napas\").",
        "en": "His name joins Mikel (Michael, the archangel who defeated Lucifer) and hats ('breath')."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mikelats-c06",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s3",
      "quote": "Bere anaiarekin batera deabruaren haitzuloan ikasi zuen, bertan kultura zabala erakutsi zieten. Baina baldintza bat zegoen: ikasketak amaitu ondoren, bietako bat leku ilun hartan geratu beharko zen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Basque, ia belajar bersama kakaknya di gua iblis, dengan syarat setelah selesai salah satu dari mereka harus tinggal di tempat gelap itu.",
        "en": "According to the Basque article, he studied with his brother in the devil's cave on condition that, when they finished, one of them must stay in that dark place."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mikelats-c07",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s3",
      "quote": "Garaia iritsitakoan, han geratu behar zena Mikelats izatea erabaki zuen zoriak, baina bere anaiak, Atarrabik, hain zuen bihotz handia, azken unean morroi bezala geratzea erabaki zuela, honela, Mikelats irten eta aske izan zen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Undian menetapkan Mikelats harus tinggal, tetapi Atarrabi yang berhati besar memilih tinggal sebagai pelayan di saat terakhir, sehingga Mikelats keluar dan bebas.",
        "en": "The lot fell on Mikelats to stay, but big-hearted Atarrabi chose at the last moment to remain as a servant, so Mikelats went out free."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mikelats-c08",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s2",
      "quote": "Tots dos van anar a l'escola que el diable, anomenat a les llegendes Etsai, regentava en una fosca cova, on van adquirir una vasta cultura.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Keduanya bersekolah di gua gelap yang dikelola iblis, yang dalam legenda disebut Etsai, dan memperoleh pengetahuan luas.",
        "en": "Both attended the school the devil, called Etsai in the legends, ran in a dark cave, where they gained wide learning."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mikelats-c09",
      "source_id": "mikelats-s1",
      "quote": "Selon Barandiaran : « Nous ne nous sommes pas arrêtés, par exemple, à considérer certains Irelu, comme ces derniers Eate, Odei et Mikelats, car une grande partie des sujets qui s'y réfèrent changent entre certains personnages et d'autres",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Barandiaran menulis bahwa ia tidak membahas khusus irelu seperti Eate, Odei, dan Mikelats karena kisah-kisah tentang mereka berpindah-pindah di antara berbagai tokoh.",
        "en": "Barandiaran wrote that he did not dwell on irelu such as Eate, Odei and Mikelats because the stories about them shift between different figures."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia fr, ca, dan eu; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mirokutana adalah roh malam Basque berwujud serigala atau anjing yang dikenal di Oiartzun dan konon tinggal di pegunungan Peñas de Aya.",
    "en": "Mirokutana is a Basque night spirit shaped like a wolf or dog, known around Oiartzun and said to live in the Peñas de Aya massif.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "mirokutana-c01",
      "mirokutana-c02"

    {
      "id": "Mirokutana adalah irelu, roh malam mitologi Basque, berwujud serigala atau anjing yang dikenal di sekitar Oiartzun dan konon tinggal di pegunungan Peñas de Aya. Ia satu dari beberapa roh berwujud anjing dalam mitologi Basque, bersama Zuzidun Txakurra dan Olanoiko zakurra, yang kekuatannya berbeda menurut tempat; sejak masa Kristen anjing semacam ini sering dianggap dirasuki roh orang yang mati tanpa pengakuan dosa.",
      "en": "Mirokutana is an irelu, a Basque night spirit, in the form of a wolf or dog, known around Oiartzun and said to live in the Peñas de Aya massif. It is one of several dog-shaped Basque spirits, with Zuzidun Txakurra and Olanoiko zakurra, whose powers vary by place; through syncretism with Christianity such dogs were often thought possessed by souls who died unconfessed.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "mirokutana-c01",
      "source_id": "mirokutana-s2",
      "quote": "Mirokutana és un geni nocturn de la mitologia basca que adopta l'aparença de llop o de gos. És conegut a la zona d'Oiartzun i es diu que viu a la zona del massís de les Peñas de Aya.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mirokutana adalah roh malam mitologi Basque yang berwujud serigala atau anjing, dikenal di daerah Oiartzun dan konon tinggal di pegunungan Peñas de Aya.",
        "en": "Mirokutana is a night spirit of Basque mythology that looks like a wolf or a dog, known around Oiartzun and said to live in the Peñas de Aya massif."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mirokutana-c02",
      "source_id": "mirokutana-s3",
      "quote": "Mirokutana otso ala txakur itxura daukan mitologiako izakia da. Gauetan ibiltzen da eta Oiartzun aldean da ezaguna.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Basque juga menyebutnya makhluk mitologi berwujud serigala atau anjing yang berkeliaran pada malam hari dan dikenal di sekitar Oiartzun.",
        "en": "The Basque article also calls it a mythical being shaped like a wolf or dog that roams at night and is known around Oiartzun."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mirokutana-c03",
      "source_id": "mirokutana-s3",
      "quote": "Mirokutana euskal mitologian agertzen diren ireluen multzoan sartzen da.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia termasuk golongan irelu dalam mitologi Basque.",
        "en": "It belongs to the group of irelu in Basque mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mirokutana-c04",
      "source_id": "mirokutana-s1",
      "quote": "Zuzidun Txakurra, Olanoiko zakurra et Mirokutana sont des génies de la mythologie basque qui ont l'aspect d'un chien. Outre le fait d'apparaitre comme des chiens, leurs pouvoirs diffèrent selon les lieux et les légendes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, Zuzidun Txakurra, Olanoiko zakurra, dan Mirokutana adalah roh Basque berwujud anjing yang kekuatannya berbeda menurut tempat dan legendanya.",
        "en": "According to the French article, Zuzidun Txakurra, Olanoiko zakurra and Mirokutana are Basque spirits shaped like dogs, whose powers differ by place and legend."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mirokutana-c05",
      "source_id": "mirokutana-s1",
      "quote": "C'est pourquoi, à partir du syncrétisme qui a généré la nouvelle religion, à de nombreuses reprises, les chiens sont pris pour des êtres possédés par les esprits de ceux qui sont morts sans confession",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lewat sinkretisme dengan agama Kristen, anjing sering dianggap makhluk yang dirasuki roh orang yang mati tanpa pengakuan dosa.",
        "en": "Through syncretism with Christianity, dogs were often taken to be possessed by the spirits of those who died unconfessed."
      }
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Monster Danau Fagua adalah makhluk rekaan bersayap kelelawar dan berwajah manusia yang disajikan sebagai nyata dalam cetakan Paris tahun 1784, konon ditangkap di danau di Chili yang sebenarnya tidak ada.",
    "en": "The Monster of Lake Fagua is an invented bat-winged, human-faced creature presented as real in Paris prints of 1784, supposedly caught in a lake in Chile that does not exist.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "monster-of-lake-fagua-c01",
      "monster-of-lake-fagua-c02",
      "monster-of-lake-fagua-c04",
      "monster-of-lake-fagua-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Pada 1784 surat kabar Courier de L'Europe dan cetakan gambar yang dijual di Paris mengabarkan bahwa monster telah ditangkap di Danau Fagua dekat Santa Fe, Chili, dan akan dipamerkan di Eropa. Ia digambarkan panjang 20 kaki, berwajah manusia, bertanduk banteng, bertelinga keledai, bersayap kelelawar, dengan tubuh bawah naga berekor dua, dan memangsa ternak pada malam hari.",
      "en": "In 1784 the Courier de L'Europe and prints sold in Paris reported that a monster had been caught in Lake Fagua near Santa Fe, Chile, and would be shown in Europe. It was described as 20 feet long, human-faced, bull-horned, donkey-eared and bat-winged, with a two-tailed dragon's lower body, preying on livestock at night.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "monster-of-lake-fagua-c01",
        "monster-of-lake-fagua-c02",
        "monster-of-lake-fagua-c03",
        "monster-of-lake-fagua-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Danau itu sebenarnya tidak ada, dan makhluk ini adalah rekaan; meski begitu \"harpy\" ini begitu populer di Paris hingga mengilhami gaya rambut, dan Annie Duprat menafsirkannya sebagai karikatur Marie Antoinette.",
      "en": "The lake did not exist and the creature was an invention; even so this 'harpy' was so popular in Paris that it inspired hairstyles, and Annie Duprat read it as a caricature of Marie Antoinette.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "monster-of-lake-fagua-c05",
        "monster-of-lake-fagua-c06",
        "monster-of-lake-fagua-c07"
      ]

      "source_id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-s1",
      "quote": "The Monster of Lake Fagua is a legendary creature, which it was claimed had been found in a lake near a place called Santa Fe, in Chile. An article about the creature was written in the Courier de L'Europe in France in 1784 stating that it had been captured and was going to be on display in Europe.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster Danau Fagua adalah makhluk legendaris yang diklaim ditemukan di danau dekat Santa Fe, Chili; sebuah artikel di Courier de L'Europe di Prancis tahun 1784 menyebutnya telah ditangkap dan akan dipamerkan di Eropa.",
        "en": "The Monster of Lake Fagua is a legendary creature claimed to have been found in a lake near Santa Fe, Chile; a 1784 article in the Courier de L'Europe in France said it had been caught and would be shown in Europe."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-c02",
      "source_id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-s1",
      "quote": "The creature is described as being 20 feet (6.1 m) long, with a human face, bull-like horns, huge donkey-like ears, lion-like teeth, bat-like wings, and the lower body of a dragon with two long pointed tails which it used to sting and attack enemies.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk itu digambarkan sepanjang 20 kaki (6,1 m), berwajah manusia, bertanduk seperti banteng, bertelinga keledai besar, bergigi singa, bersayap kelelawar, dan bertubuh bawah naga dengan dua ekor panjang runcing untuk menyengat musuh.",
        "en": "The creature is described as 20 feet (6.1 m) long, with a human face, bull-like horns, huge donkey ears, lion's teeth, bat wings, and a dragon's lower body with two long pointed tails for stinging enemies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-c03",
      "source_id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-s1",
      "quote": "It was said to be nocturnal and its diet consisted of livestock, like cattle and pigs.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon aktif pada malam hari dan memakan ternak seperti sapi dan babi.",
        "en": "It was said to be nocturnal and to eat livestock such as cattle and pigs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-c04",
      "source_id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-s2",
      "quote": "Le monstre du lac Fagua est une créature lacustre imaginaire, présentée comme réelle sur plusieurs estampes vendues à Paris en octobre 1784.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, monster danau Fagua adalah makhluk danau khayalan yang disajikan sebagai nyata dalam beberapa cetakan gambar yang dijual di Paris pada Oktober 1784.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the monster of Lake Fagua is an imaginary lake creature presented as real in several prints sold in Paris in October 1784."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-c05",
      "source_id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-s2",
      "quote": "L'engouement pour cette « harpie » est tel qu'il inspire de nouvelles fantaisies aux coiffeurs des élégantes, qui appréciaient alors les coiffures extravagantes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kegemaran pada \"harpy\" ini begitu besar sehingga mengilhami gaya rambut baru para perempuan modis.",
        "en": "The craze for this 'harpy' was such that it inspired new fancies among fashionable ladies' hairdressers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-c06",
      "source_id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-s3",
      "quote": "ところが、この説明に登場する「ファグア湖」は実在せず、架空の地名であるため、生物の報告も同様に疑わしい。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Jepang mencatat bahwa \"Danau Fagua\" tidak ada dan merupakan nama tempat rekaan, sehingga laporan tentang makhluk itu juga meragukan.",
        "en": "The Japanese article notes that 'Lake Fagua' does not exist and is an invented place name, so the creature report is equally doubtful."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-c07",
      "source_id": "monster-of-lake-fagua-s3",
      "quote": "アニー・デュプラはこれをマリー・アントワネットの風刺画であると解釈した。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Annie Duprat menafsirkannya sebagai karikatur Marie Antoinette.",
        "en": "Annie Duprat interpreted it as a caricature of Marie Antoinette."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",

    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Monster Ravenna adalah kelahiran aneh yang dilaporkan di Ravenna pada 1512, bertanduk dan bersayap kelelawar, yang ditafsirkan sebagai pertanda Pertempuran Ravenna.",
    "en": "The Monster of Ravenna is a monstrous birth reported at Ravenna in 1512, horned and bat-winged, read as an omen of the Battle of Ravenna.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "monster-of-ravenna-c01",
      "monster-of-ravenna-c02",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Monster Ravenna adalah kelahiran aneh yang dilaporkan di dekat Ravenna pada awal 1512, mungkin apokrif. Catatan harian Tedallini menyebutnya anak biarawati dan biarawan yang bertanduk, bertanda YXV di dada, dan berkaki aneh; Luca Landucci menambahkan sayap kelelawar, mata di lutut, dan kaki bercakar elang. Bayi itu ditelantarkan hingga mati atas perintah Paus.",
      "en": "The Monster of Ravenna is a monstrous birth reported near Ravenna in early 1512, possibly apocryphal. Tedallini's diary calls it the child of a nun and a friar, horned, marked YXV on the chest and with strange legs; Luca Landucci added bat wings, an eye on the knee and an eagle's clawed foot. The infant was left to die on the Pope's order.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "monster-of-ravenna-c01",
        "monster-of-ravenna-c04",
        "monster-of-ravenna-c05",
        "monster-of-ravenna-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia paling sering ditafsirkan sebagai pertanda Pertempuran Ravenna (1512), dan tanda di dadanya dibaca sebagai pesan moral; tahun kelahirannya pun berbeda antara Lycosthenes (1511) dan Boaistuau (1512). Pihak Katolik maupun Protestan memakainya sebagai simbol. Konsensus medis modern menganggapnya anak dengan kelainan bawaan berat.",
      "en": "It was most often read as an omen of the Battle of Ravenna (1512), and the signs on its chest as a moral message; even its birth year differs between Lycosthenes (1511) and Boaistuau (1512). Catholic and Protestant opponents both used it as a symbol. Modern medical consensus sees a child with a severe congenital disorder.",

      "author": null,

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c01",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s1",
      "quote": "The Monster of Ravenna was a possibly apocryphal late Renaissance-era monstrous birth whose appearance in early 1512 near the city of Ravenna was widely reported in contemporary European pamphlets and diaries.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Monster Ravenna adalah kelahiran aneh dari akhir masa Renaisans, mungkin apokrif, yang kemunculannya di dekat Ravenna awal 1512 banyak dilaporkan dalam pamflet dan catatan harian Eropa masa itu.",
        "en": "The Monster of Ravenna was a possibly apocryphal monstrous birth of the late Renaissance whose appearance near Ravenna in early 1512 was widely reported in European pamphlets and diaries of the time."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c02",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s1",
      "quote": "Images of its grotesque features were interpreted symbolically by opponents of both the Catholic Church and the Protestant Reformation, although a more common explanation at the time was that the beast was an omen regarding the outcome of the Battle of Ravenna (1512).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gambaran rupanya yang aneh ditafsirkan secara simbolis oleh penentang Gereja Katolik maupun Reformasi Protestan, tetapi penjelasan yang lebih umum saat itu ialah bahwa makhluk itu pertanda hasil Pertempuran Ravenna (1512).",
        "en": "Its grotesque features were read symbolically by opponents of both the Catholic Church and the Protestant Reformation, though the commoner explanation then was that it was an omen of the Battle of Ravenna (1512)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c03",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s1",
      "quote": "Modern medical consensus identifies the monster as a child with some variety of severe congenital disorder.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konsensus medis modern mengenalinya sebagai anak dengan kelainan bawaan berat.",
        "en": "Modern medical consensus identifies the monster as a child with a severe congenital disorder."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c04",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s1",
      "quote": "According to his account, the child was said to have been born of a nun and a friar, and was marked by a horned head, the letters YXV on its chest, and with one leg hairy and cloven-hoofed while the other leg's midsection grew a human eye.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut catatan harian Sebastiano di Branca Tedallini, anak itu konon lahir dari seorang biarawati dan biarawan, berkepala bertanduk, bertanda huruf YXV di dadanya, dengan satu kaki berbulu berkuku belah dan kaki lain bermata manusia di tengahnya.",
        "en": "According to the diary of Sebastiano di Branca Tedallini, the child was said to be born of a nun and a friar, with a horned head, the letters YXV on its chest, one hairy cloven-hoofed leg and a human eye in the middle of the other."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c05",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s1",
      "quote": "Landucci described it to possess features such as a single horn, the wings of a bat, hermaphroditic genitalia, an eye on its right knee and a clawed left foot like an eagle's.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Luca Landucci menggambarkannya bertanduk satu, bersayap kelelawar, berkelamin ganda, bermata di lutut kanan, dan berkaki kiri bercakar seperti elang.",
        "en": "Luca Landucci described a single horn, bat wings, hermaphrodite genitals, an eye on the right knee and a clawed left foot like an eagle's."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c06",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s1",
      "quote": "In medieval Europe, it was common practice for malformed infants, especially those with little chance of survival, to be abandoned and left to die by starvation; this was done in Ravenna on the order of the Pope.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Eropa abad pertengahan bayi cacat yang kecil peluang hidupnya biasa ditelantarkan hingga mati kelaparan, dan di Ravenna hal ini dilakukan atas perintah Paus.",
        "en": "In medieval Europe malformed infants with little chance of survival were commonly left to starve, and in Ravenna this was done on the Pope's order."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c07",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s2",
      "quote": "Le monstre serait né en 1511 à Ravenne, selon Lycosthènes, année précédant la bataille, ou le jour même (11 avril 1512), selon Boaistuau.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, monster itu lahir pada 1511 menurut Lycosthenes, setahun sebelum pertempuran, atau pada hari pertempuran itu sendiri (11 April 1512) menurut Boaistuau.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the monster was born in 1511 according to Lycosthenes, the year before the battle, or on the very day (11 April 1512) according to Boaistuau."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c08",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s2",
      "quote": "Les Italiens n'ont pas suivi les signes inscrits sur la poitrine du monstre : l'upsilon (Υ) signifiant désir de vertu, et la croix au-dessous l'enseignement du Christ.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tanda di dadanya ditafsirkan sebagai upsilon (Υ), lambang keinginan akan kebajikan, dan salib di bawahnya ajaran Kristus, yang tidak diikuti orang Italia.",
        "en": "The signs on its chest were read as an upsilon (Υ), the desire for virtue, and a cross below it, Christ's teaching, which the Italians failed to follow."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "monster-of-ravenna-c09",
      "source_id": "monster-of-ravenna-s3",
      "quote": "Современиот медицински консензус го идентификувал чудовиштето како дете со различни тешки вродени нарушувања.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Makedonia juga menyebut konsensus medis modern mengenalinya sebagai anak dengan kelainan bawaan berat.",
        "en": "The Macedonian article likewise says modern medical consensus identifies it as a child with severe congenital disorders."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Tahun kelahiran",
```

```json
    {
      "id": "Muscaliet adalah hewan bestiari yang dijelaskan dalam Bestiaire Pierre de Beauvais: tubuh kelinci kecil, kaki dan ekor tupai, telinga musang, moncong tikus tanah, gigi babi hutan, dan kulit lebat seperti babi. Ia memanjat pohon, merusak daun dan buah, bersarang di bawah akar, dan panas tubuhnya mengeringkan pohon sampai mati; namanya mungkin dari bahasa Latin calor, \"panas\".",
      "en": "The muscaliet is a bestiary animal described in Pierre de Beauvais's Bestiaire: a small hare's body, a squirrel's legs and tail, a weasel's ears, a mole's snout, a boar's teeth and a pig's bristly skin. It climbs trees, ruins leaves and fruit, nests under the roots, and its body heat dries the tree to death; its name may come from Latin calor, 'heat'.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "muscaliet-c01",
      "source_id": "muscaliet-s1",
      "quote": "The muscaliet is an animal from Medieval bestiaries. Pierre de Beauvais ... 13th century Bestiaire includes a description of the animal, comparing it in various aspects to hares, squirrels, weasels, moles and pigs.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Muscaliet adalah hewan dari bestiari Abad Pertengahan; Bestiaire karya Pierre de Beauvais abad ke-13 menggambarkannya dengan membandingkan bagian-bagiannya dengan kelinci, tupai, musang, tikus tanah, dan babi.",
        "en": "The muscaliet is an animal of Medieval bestiaries; Pierre de Beauvais's 13th-century Bestiaire describes it by comparing it in various ways to hares, squirrels, weasels, moles and pigs."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muscaliet-c02",
      "source_id": "muscaliet-s2",
      "quote": "Le Bestiaire de Pierre de Beauvais, datant du XIIe siècle, comprend une description de l'animal qui a le corps d'un lièvre (mais plus petit), les pattes et la queue d'un écureuil, les oreilles d'une belette et le museau d'une taupe. Il a des dents de sanglier et sa peau est touffue comme celle d'un porc.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, Bestiaire Pierre de Beauvais dari abad ke-12 menggambarkannya bertubuh kelinci yang lebih kecil, berkaki dan berekor tupai, bertelinga musang, bermoncong tikus tanah, bergigi babi hutan, dan berkulit lebat seperti babi.",
        "en": "According to the French article, Pierre de Beauvais's 12th-century Bestiaire describes it with a hare's body but smaller, a squirrel's legs and tail, a weasel's ears, a mole's snout, a boar's teeth and a pig's bristly skin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muscaliet-c03",
      "source_id": "muscaliet-s2",
      "quote": "Il grimpe aux arbres et saute de branche en branche à l'aide de sa queue. Lorsqu'il grimpe dans un arbre, la muscaliet détruit le feuillage et les fruits. Sous les racines d'un arbre, il fait son nid. Sa chaleur naturelle fait que l'arbre se dessèche et meurt",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia memanjat pohon, melompat antardahan dengan bantuan ekornya, merusak daun dan buah, bersarang di bawah akar, dan panas tubuhnya membuat pohon kering lalu mati.",
        "en": "It climbs trees and leaps from branch to branch with its tail, destroys leaves and fruit, nests under the roots, and its natural heat dries the tree until it dies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muscaliet-c04",
      "source_id": "muscaliet-s2",
      "quote": "Mais le muscaliet est l'Orgueil, ses dents acérées sont les mots tranchants qu'apporte la Cruauté, et ses pieds montrent que la cruauté est tenace.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam tafsiran moral Beauvais, pohon adalah manusia dan muscaliet adalah Kesombongan, giginya kata-kata tajam Kekejaman, dan kakinya menunjukkan kekejaman yang gigih.",
        "en": "In Beauvais's moral reading the tree is a person and the muscaliet is Pride, its sharp teeth the cutting words of Cruelty, its feet showing cruelty's persistence."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muscaliet-c05",
      "source_id": "muscaliet-s2",
      "quote": "Peut-être que le -caliet vient du latin calor et a été choisi pour décrire une créature chaude par nature.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bagian -caliet mungkin berasal dari bahasa Latin calor, \"panas\", untuk menggambarkan makhluk yang panas secara alami.",
        "en": "The -caliet may come from Latin calor, 'heat', chosen for a naturally hot creature."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "muscaliet-c06",
      "source_id": "muscaliet-s3",
      "quote": "Een muscaliet is een fruitetend fabeldier dat het lichaam heeft van een haas (maar dan kleiner), de benen en de staart van een eekhoorn, de oren van een wezel en de snuit van een mol.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Belanda menyebut muscaliet hewan dongeng pemakan buah bertubuh kelinci yang lebih kecil, berkaki dan berekor tupai, bertelinga musang, dan bermoncong tikus tanah.",
        "en": "The Dutch article calls the muscaliet a fruit-eating fabulous animal with a smaller hare's body, a squirrel's legs and tail, a weasel's ears and a mole's snout."
      }
    }
```
